===== lib\features\analytics\application\controllers\playback_analytics_controller.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import 'dart:async';

import '../../../player/application/controllers/playback_coordinator.dart';
import '../../../player/domain/models/playback_state.dart';
import '../../../player/domain/models/playback_transition_cause.dart';
import '../../../player/domain/models/playback_track.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/playback_session.dart';
import '../services/listening_session_start.dart';
import '../services/listening_session_tracker.dart';

typedef PlaybackAnalyticsNow = DateTime Function();
typedef PlaybackAnalyticsUserIdProvider = String? Function();
typedef PlaybackAnalyticsErrorHandler =
    void Function(Object error, StackTrace stackTrace);

class PlaybackAnalyticsController {
  PlaybackAnalyticsController({
    required PlaybackCoordinator playbackCoordinator,
    required ListeningSessionTracker tracker,
    Duration checkpointInterval = const Duration(seconds: 5),
    PlaybackAnalyticsNow? now,
    PlaybackAnalyticsUserIdProvider? userIdProvider,
    PlaybackAnalyticsErrorHandler? onError,
  }) : _playbackCoordinator = playbackCoordinator,
       _tracker = tracker,
       _checkpointInterval = checkpointInterval,
       _now = now ?? DateTime.now,
       _userIdProvider = userIdProvider,
       _onError = onError {
    _subscription = _playbackCoordinator.states.listen(_enqueueState);

    _enqueueState(_playbackCoordinator.currentState);
  }

  final PlaybackCoordinator _playbackCoordinator;
  final ListeningSessionTracker _tracker;
  final Duration _checkpointInterval;
  final PlaybackAnalyticsNow _now;
  final PlaybackAnalyticsUserIdProvider? _userIdProvider;
  final PlaybackAnalyticsErrorHandler? _onError;

  late final StreamSubscription<PlaybackState> _subscription;

  PlaybackState? _previousState;
  DateTime? _lastCheckpointAt;

  Future<void> _pending = Future<void>.value();

  bool _disposed = false;

  Object? _lastError;

  Object? get lastError => _lastError;

  Future<void> get settled => _pending;

  Future<void> attachActiveSessionToUser(String userId) {
    if (_disposed) {
      return Future<void>.error(
        StateError('Playback analytics controller is disposed.'),
      );
    }

    final completer = Completer<void>();

    _pending = _pending.then((_) async {
      try {
        await _tracker.attachCurrentSessionToUser(userId);

        completer.complete();
      } catch (error, stackTrace) {
        _lastError = error;
        _onError?.call(error, stackTrace);

        completer.completeError(error, stackTrace);
      }
    });

    return completer.future;
  }

  void _enqueueState(PlaybackState state) {
    if (_disposed) {
      return;
    }

    _pending = _pending.then((_) => _handleState(state)).catchError((
      Object error,
      StackTrace stackTrace,
    ) {
      _lastError = error;
      _onError?.call(error, stackTrace);
    });
  }

  Future<void> _handleState(PlaybackState state) async {
    if (_disposed) {
      return;
    }

    final previous = _previousState;
    final track = state.queue.currentTrack;

    if (_tracker.hasActiveSession) {
      final activeSongId = _tracker.currentSession!.songId;

      if (track == null || track.songId != activeSongId) {
        await _tracker.end(
          reason: _endReasonForTrackChange(previous, state),
          position:
              previous?.position ?? _tracker.currentSession!.finalPosition,
          trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(previous),
        );

        _lastCheckpointAt = null;
      } else if (_looksLikeCompletedRestart(previous, state)) {
        await _tracker.end(
          reason: PlaybackSessionEndReason.completed,
          position: previous!.position,
          trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(previous),
        );

        _lastCheckpointAt = null;
      }
    }

    if (track == null) {
      _previousState = state;
      return;
    }

    if (!_tracker.hasActiveSession) {
      if (state.status == PlaybackStatus.playing) {
        await _startForTrack(track, state);
      }

      _previousState = state;
      return;
    }

    switch (state.status) {
      case PlaybackStatus.playing:
        await _handlePlaying(state);
        break;

      case PlaybackStatus.paused:
      case PlaybackStatus.ready:
      case PlaybackStatus.loading:
        if (_tracker.isPlaying) {
          await _tracker.markPaused(
            position: state.position,
            trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(state),
          );
        }
        break;

      case PlaybackStatus.completed:
        await _tracker.end(
          reason: PlaybackSessionEndReason.completed,
          position: state.position,
          trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(state),
        );
        _lastCheckpointAt = null;
        break;

      case PlaybackStatus.failed:
        await _tracker.end(
          reason: PlaybackSessionEndReason.failed,
          position: state.position,
          trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(state),
        );
        _lastCheckpointAt = null;
        break;

      case PlaybackStatus.idle:
        await _tracker.end(
          reason: PlaybackSessionEndReason.stopped,
          position: state.position,
          trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(state),
        );
        _lastCheckpointAt = null;
        break;
    }

    _previousState = state;
  }

  Future<void> _startForTrack(PlaybackTrack track, PlaybackState state) async {
    final albumEditionId = track.albumEditionId;

    if (albumEditionId == null || albumEditionId.trim().isEmpty) {
      throw StateError(
        'Playback track ${track.songId} is missing an album edition id.',
      );
    }

    await _tracker.start(
      ListeningSessionStart(
        userId: _currentUserId(),
        songId: track.songId,
        artistId: track.artistId,
        albumId: track.albumId,
        albumEditionId: albumEditionId,
        songTitle: track.title,
        artistName: track.artistName,
        albumTitle: track.albumTitle,
        albumEditionTitle: track.albumEditionTitle?.trim().isNotEmpty == true
            ? track.albumEditionTitle!
            : track.albumTitle,
        trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(state) ?? track.duration,
      ),
      initialPosition: state.position,
      playing: true,
    );

    _lastCheckpointAt = _utcNow();
  }

  Future<void> _handlePlaying(PlaybackState state) async {
    if (!_tracker.isPlaying) {
      await _tracker.markPlaying(
        position: state.position,
        trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(state),
      );

      _lastCheckpointAt = _utcNow();
      return;
    }

    final now = _utcNow();
    final lastCheckpointAt = _lastCheckpointAt;

    if (lastCheckpointAt == null ||
        now.difference(lastCheckpointAt) >= _checkpointInterval) {
      await _tracker.checkpoint(
        position: state.position,
        trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(state),
      );

      _lastCheckpointAt = now;
    }
  }

  PlaybackSessionEndReason _endReasonForTrackChange(
    PlaybackState? previous,
    PlaybackState current,
  ) {
    switch (current.transitionCause) {
      case PlaybackTransitionCause.skipNext:
      case PlaybackTransitionCause.skipPrevious:
      case PlaybackTransitionCause.skipToIndex:
        return PlaybackSessionEndReason.skipped;

      case PlaybackTransitionCause.automatic:
        return PlaybackSessionEndReason.completed;

      case null:
        break;
    }

    if (previous == null) {
      return PlaybackSessionEndReason.replaced;
    }

    if (previous.status == PlaybackStatus.completed ||
        _wasNearTrackEnd(previous)) {
      return PlaybackSessionEndReason.completed;
    }

    return PlaybackSessionEndReason.skipped;
  }

  bool _looksLikeCompletedRestart(
    PlaybackState? previous,
    PlaybackState current,
  ) {
    if (previous == null) {
      return false;
    }

    final previousTrack = previous.queue.currentTrack;
    final currentTrack = current.queue.currentTrack;

    if (previousTrack == null ||
        currentTrack == null ||
        previousTrack.songId != currentTrack.songId) {
      return false;
    }

    if (!_wasNearTrackEnd(previous)) {
      return false;
    }

    return current.status == PlaybackStatus.playing &&
        current.position <= const Duration(seconds: 2) &&
        current.position < previous.position;
  }

  bool _wasNearTrackEnd(PlaybackState state) {
    final duration = _resolvedDuration(state);

    if (duration == null || duration <= Duration.zero) {
      return false;
    }

    final remaining = duration - state.position;

    return remaining <= const Duration(seconds: 2);
  }

  Duration? _resolvedDuration(PlaybackState? state) {
    if (state == null) {
      return null;
    }

    if (state.duration > Duration.zero) {
      return state.duration;
    }

    final trackDuration = state.queue.currentTrack?.duration;

    if (trackDuration != null && trackDuration > Duration.zero) {
      return trackDuration;
    }

    return null;
  }

  String? _currentUserId() {
    final userId = _userIdProvider?.call()?.trim();

    if (userId == null || userId.isEmpty) {
      return null;
    }

    return userId;
  }

  DateTime _utcNow() => _now().toUtc();

  Future<void> dispose() async {
    if (_disposed) {
      return;
    }

    _disposed = true;

    await _subscription.cancel();
    await _pending;

    if (_tracker.hasActiveSession) {
      final previous = _previousState;

      await _tracker.end(
        reason: PlaybackSessionEndReason.interrupted,
        position: previous?.position ?? _tracker.currentSession!.finalPosition,
        trackDuration: _resolvedDuration(previous),
      );
    }
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\models\analytics_behavior_metrics.dart =====
import 'analytics_peak_day.dart';
import 'analytics_period.dart';
import 'analytics_time_window.dart';

class AnalyticsBehaviorMetrics {
  const AnalyticsBehaviorMetrics({
    required this.period,
    required this.window,
    required this.sessionCount,
    required this.endedSessionCount,
    required this.completedSessionCount,
    required this.skippedSessionCount,
    required this.stoppedSessionCount,
    required this.replacedSessionCount,
    required this.interruptedSessionCount,
    required this.failedSessionCount,
    required this.firstSessionStartedAt,
    required this.lastSessionStartedAt,
    required this.activeQualifiedDayCount,
    required this.averageQualifiedStreamsPerActiveDay,
    required this.averageQualifiedStreamsPerCalendarDay,
    required this.peakDay,
  });

  final AnalyticsPeriod period;
  final AnalyticsTimeWindow window;

  final int sessionCount;
  final int endedSessionCount;

  final int completedSessionCount;
  final int skippedSessionCount;
  final int stoppedSessionCount;
  final int replacedSessionCount;
  final int interruptedSessionCount;
  final int failedSessionCount;

  final DateTime? firstSessionStartedAt;
  final DateTime? lastSessionStartedAt;

  final int activeQualifiedDayCount;
  final double averageQualifiedStreamsPerActiveDay;
  final double averageQualifiedStreamsPerCalendarDay;

  final AnalyticsPeakDay? peakDay;

  double get completionRate {
    if (endedSessionCount == 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    return completedSessionCount / endedSessionCount;
  }

  double get skipRate {
    if (endedSessionCount == 0) {
      return 0;
    }

    return skippedSessionCount / endedSessionCount;
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\models\analytics_time_window.dart =====
class AnalyticsTimeWindow {
  const AnalyticsTimeWindow({required this.startUtc, required this.endUtc});

  final DateTime? startUtc;
  final DateTime? endUtc;

  bool get isLifetime => startUtc == null && endUtc == null;

  bool contains(DateTime instant) {
    final utc = instant.toUtc();
    final start = startUtc;
    final end = endUtc;

    if (start != null && utc.isBefore(start)) {
      return false;
    }

    if (end != null && !utc.isBefore(end)) {
      return false;
    }

    return true;
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\analytics_account_attachment_service.dart =====
import '../../domain/entities/qualified_play.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';

class AnalyticsAccountAttachmentResult {
  const AnalyticsAccountAttachmentResult({
    required this.sessionsAttached,
    required this.qualifiedPlaysAttached,
  });

  final int sessionsAttached;
  final int qualifiedPlaysAttached;
}

class AnalyticsAccountAttachmentService {
  const AnalyticsAccountAttachmentService({
    required this._playbackSessions,
    required this._qualifiedPlays,
  });

  final PlaybackSessionRepository _playbackSessions;
  final QualifiedPlayRepository _qualifiedPlays;

  Future<AnalyticsAccountAttachmentResult> attachUnownedToUser(
    String userId,
  ) async {
    final normalizedUserId = userId.trim();

    if (normalizedUserId.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(userId, 'userId', 'User id must not be blank.');
    }

    final sessions = await _playbackSessions.getAll();

    final unownedSessions = sessions
        .where((session) => session.userId == null)
        .toList(growable: false);

    if (unownedSessions.isEmpty) {
      return const AnalyticsAccountAttachmentResult(
        sessionsAttached: 0,
        qualifiedPlaysAttached: 0,
      );
    }

    final sessionIds = unownedSessions
        .map((session) => session.id)
        .toList(growable: false);

    final plays = await _qualifiedPlays.getByPlaybackSessionIds(sessionIds);

    _validateQualifiedPlayOwnership(plays: plays, userId: normalizedUserId);

    for (final session in unownedSessions) {
      await _playbackSessions.save(session.copyWith(userId: normalizedUserId));
    }

    var qualifiedPlaysAttached = 0;

    for (final play in plays) {
      if (play.userId != null) {
        continue;
      }

      await _qualifiedPlays.save(play.copyWith(userId: normalizedUserId));

      qualifiedPlaysAttached++;
    }

    return AnalyticsAccountAttachmentResult(
      sessionsAttached: unownedSessions.length,
      qualifiedPlaysAttached: qualifiedPlaysAttached,
    );
  }

  void _validateQualifiedPlayOwnership({
    required List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
    required String userId,
  }) {
    for (final play in plays) {
      final owner = play.userId;

      if (owner != null && owner != userId) {
        throw StateError(
          'Qualified play ${play.id} belongs to a different user.',
        );
      }
    }
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\analytics_artist_listening_duration_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../domain/entities/playback_listening_segment.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/playback_session.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_listening_segment_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../models/analytics_period.dart';
import '../models/analytics_time_window.dart';
import 'analytics_time_window_resolver.dart';

class AnalyticsArtistListeningDurationService {
  const AnalyticsArtistListeningDurationService({
    required PlaybackSessionRepository playbackSessions,
    required PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository listeningSegments,
    AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver timeWindowResolver =
        const AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver(),
  }) : _playbackSessions = playbackSessions,
       _listeningSegments = listeningSegments,
       _timeWindowResolver = timeWindowResolver;

  final PlaybackSessionRepository _playbackSessions;
  final PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository _listeningSegments;
  final AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver _timeWindowResolver;

  Future<Map<String, Duration>> load({
    required AnalyticsPeriod period,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
  }) async {
    final window = _timeWindowResolver.resolve(
      period: period,
      referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
    );

    final segments = await _listeningSegments.getAll(
      startedBefore: window.endUtc,
    );

    final sessionCache = <String, PlaybackSession?>{};
    final totalsMilliseconds = <String, int>{};

    for (final segment in segments) {
      final duration = _durationInsideWindow(segment: segment, window: window);

      if (duration <= Duration.zero) {
        continue;
      }

      final sessionId = segment.playbackSessionId;

      if (!sessionCache.containsKey(sessionId)) {
        sessionCache[sessionId] = await _playbackSessions.getById(sessionId);
      }

      final session = sessionCache[sessionId];

      if (session == null) {
        continue;
      }

      totalsMilliseconds.update(
        session.artistId,
        (current) => current + duration.inMilliseconds,
        ifAbsent: () => duration.inMilliseconds,
      );
    }

    return Map<String, Duration>.unmodifiable(
      totalsMilliseconds.map(
        (artistId, milliseconds) =>
            MapEntry(artistId, Duration(milliseconds: milliseconds)),
      ),
    );
  }

  Duration _durationInsideWindow({
    required PlaybackListeningSegment segment,
    required AnalyticsTimeWindow window,
  }) {
    final segmentStart = segment.startedAt.toUtc();
    final segmentEnd = _truthfulEndpoint(segment);

    if (!segmentEnd.isAfter(segmentStart)) {
      return Duration.zero;
    }

    var overlapStart = segmentStart;
    var overlapEnd = segmentEnd;

    final windowStart = window.startUtc;
    final windowEnd = window.endUtc;

    if (windowStart != null && overlapStart.isBefore(windowStart)) {
      overlapStart = windowStart;
    }

    if (windowEnd != null && overlapEnd.isAfter(windowEnd)) {
      overlapEnd = windowEnd;
    }

    if (!overlapEnd.isAfter(overlapStart)) {
      return Duration.zero;
    }

    return overlapEnd.difference(overlapStart);
  }

  DateTime _truthfulEndpoint(PlaybackListeningSegment segment) {
    final endedAt = segment.endedAt;

    if (endedAt != null) {
      return endedAt.toUtc();
    }

    final listenedDuration = segment.listenedDuration;

    if (listenedDuration <= Duration.zero) {
      return segment.startedAt.toUtc();
    }

    return segment.startedAt.toUtc().add(listenedDuration);
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\analytics_behavior_metrics_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../domain/entities/playback_session.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/qualified_play.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';
import '../models/analytics_behavior_metrics.dart';
import '../models/analytics_peak_day.dart';
import '../models/analytics_period.dart';
import '../models/analytics_time_window.dart';
import 'analytics_time_window_resolver.dart';

class AnalyticsBehaviorMetricsService {
  const AnalyticsBehaviorMetricsService({
    required PlaybackSessionRepository playbackSessions,
    required QualifiedPlayRepository qualifiedPlays,
    AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver timeWindowResolver =
        const AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver(),
  }) : _playbackSessions = playbackSessions,
       _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays,
       _timeWindowResolver = timeWindowResolver;

  final PlaybackSessionRepository _playbackSessions;
  final QualifiedPlayRepository _qualifiedPlays;
  final AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver _timeWindowResolver;

  Future<AnalyticsBehaviorMetrics> load({
    required AnalyticsPeriod period,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
  }) async {
    final window = _timeWindowResolver.resolve(
      period: period,
      referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
    );

    final sessionsFuture = _playbackSessions.getAll(
      startedAtOrAfter: window.startUtc,
      startedBefore: window.endUtc,
    );

    final playsFuture = _qualifiedPlays.getAll(
      qualifiedAtOrAfter: window.startUtc,
      qualifiedBefore: window.endUtc,
    );

    final sessions = await sessionsFuture;
    final plays = await playsFuture;

    return _build(
      period: period,
      window: window,
      referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
      sessions: sessions,
      plays: plays,
    );
  }

  AnalyticsBehaviorMetrics _build({
    required AnalyticsPeriod period,
    required AnalyticsTimeWindow window,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
    required List<PlaybackSession> sessions,
    required List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
  }) {
    var endedSessionCount = 0;

    var completedSessionCount = 0;
    var skippedSessionCount = 0;
    var stoppedSessionCount = 0;
    var replacedSessionCount = 0;
    var interruptedSessionCount = 0;
    var failedSessionCount = 0;

    DateTime? firstSessionStartedAt;
    DateTime? lastSessionStartedAt;

    for (final session in sessions) {
      final startedAt = session.startedAt.toUtc();

      if (firstSessionStartedAt == null ||
          startedAt.isBefore(firstSessionStartedAt)) {
        firstSessionStartedAt = startedAt;
      }

      if (lastSessionStartedAt == null ||
          startedAt.isAfter(lastSessionStartedAt)) {
        lastSessionStartedAt = startedAt;
      }

      final reason = session.endReason;

      if (!session.isEnded || reason == null) {
        continue;
      }

      endedSessionCount++;

      switch (reason) {
        case PlaybackSessionEndReason.completed:
          completedSessionCount++;
          break;

        case PlaybackSessionEndReason.skipped:
          skippedSessionCount++;
          break;

        case PlaybackSessionEndReason.stopped:
          stoppedSessionCount++;
          break;

        case PlaybackSessionEndReason.replaced:
          replacedSessionCount++;
          break;

        case PlaybackSessionEndReason.interrupted:
          interruptedSessionCount++;
          break;

        case PlaybackSessionEndReason.failed:
          failedSessionCount++;
          break;
      }
    }

    final qualifiedByLocalDay = <DateTime, int>{};

    for (final play in plays) {
      final local = play.qualifiedAt.toLocal();

      final localDate = DateTime(local.year, local.month, local.day);

      qualifiedByLocalDay.update(
        localDate,
        (count) => count + 1,
        ifAbsent: () => 1,
      );
    }

    AnalyticsPeakDay? peakDay;

    for (final entry in qualifiedByLocalDay.entries) {
      final currentPeak = peakDay;

      if (currentPeak == null ||
          entry.value > currentPeak.qualifiedStreamCount ||
          (entry.value == currentPeak.qualifiedStreamCount &&
              entry.key.isBefore(currentPeak.localDate))) {
        peakDay = AnalyticsPeakDay(
          localDate: entry.key,
          qualifiedStreamCount: entry.value,
        );
      }
    }

    final activeQualifiedDayCount = qualifiedByLocalDay.length;

    final averageQualifiedStreamsPerActiveDay = activeQualifiedDayCount == 0
        ? 0.0
        : plays.length / activeQualifiedDayCount;

    final calendarDayCount = _calendarDayCount(
      period: period,
      referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
      qualifiedByLocalDay: qualifiedByLocalDay,
    );

    final averageQualifiedStreamsPerCalendarDay =
        plays.isEmpty || calendarDayCount == 0
        ? 0.0
        : plays.length / calendarDayCount;

    return AnalyticsBehaviorMetrics(
      period: period,
      window: window,
      sessionCount: sessions.length,
      endedSessionCount: endedSessionCount,
      completedSessionCount: completedSessionCount,
      skippedSessionCount: skippedSessionCount,
      stoppedSessionCount: stoppedSessionCount,
      replacedSessionCount: replacedSessionCount,
      interruptedSessionCount: interruptedSessionCount,
      failedSessionCount: failedSessionCount,
      firstSessionStartedAt: firstSessionStartedAt,
      lastSessionStartedAt: lastSessionStartedAt,
      activeQualifiedDayCount: activeQualifiedDayCount,
      averageQualifiedStreamsPerActiveDay: averageQualifiedStreamsPerActiveDay,
      averageQualifiedStreamsPerCalendarDay:
          averageQualifiedStreamsPerCalendarDay,
      peakDay: peakDay,
    );
  }

  int _calendarDayCount({
    required AnalyticsPeriod period,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
    required Map<DateTime, int> qualifiedByLocalDay,
  }) {
    switch (period) {
      case AnalyticsPeriod.day:
        return 1;

      case AnalyticsPeriod.week:
        return 7;

      case AnalyticsPeriod.month:
        final start = DateTime.utc(referenceLocal.year, referenceLocal.month);

        final end = DateTime.utc(referenceLocal.year, referenceLocal.month + 1);

        return end.difference(start).inDays;

      case AnalyticsPeriod.year:
        final start = DateTime.utc(referenceLocal.year);
        final end = DateTime.utc(referenceLocal.year + 1);

        return end.difference(start).inDays;

      case AnalyticsPeriod.lifetime:
        if (qualifiedByLocalDay.isEmpty) {
          return 0;
        }

        var firstQualifiedLocalDay = qualifiedByLocalDay.keys.first;

        for (final localDay in qualifiedByLocalDay.keys.skip(1)) {
          if (localDay.isBefore(firstQualifiedLocalDay)) {
            firstQualifiedLocalDay = localDay;
          }
        }

        final referenceLocalDay = DateTime(
          referenceLocal.year,
          referenceLocal.month,
          referenceLocal.day,
        );

        if (firstQualifiedLocalDay.isAfter(referenceLocalDay)) {
          return 1;
        }

        final firstUtcDate = DateTime.utc(
          firstQualifiedLocalDay.year,
          firstQualifiedLocalDay.month,
          firstQualifiedLocalDay.day,
        );

        final referenceUtcDate = DateTime.utc(
          referenceLocalDay.year,
          referenceLocalDay.month,
          referenceLocalDay.day,
        );

        return referenceUtcDate.difference(firstUtcDate).inDays + 1;
    }
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\analytics_entity_ranking_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../domain/entities/qualified_play.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';
import '../models/analytics_entity_rankings.dart';
import '../models/analytics_period.dart';
import '../models/analytics_ranked_entity.dart';
import 'analytics_time_window_resolver.dart';

class AnalyticsEntityRankingService {
  const AnalyticsEntityRankingService({
    required QualifiedPlayRepository qualifiedPlays,
    AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver timeWindowResolver =
        const AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver(),
  }) : _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays,
       _timeWindowResolver = timeWindowResolver;

  final QualifiedPlayRepository _qualifiedPlays;
  final AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver _timeWindowResolver;

  Future<AnalyticsEntityRankings> load({
    required AnalyticsPeriod period,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
    int? limit,
  }) async {
    if (limit != null && limit <= 0) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        limit,
        'limit',
        'Must be greater than zero when provided.',
      );
    }

    final window = _timeWindowResolver.resolve(
      period: period,
      referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
    );

    final plays = await _qualifiedPlays.getAll(
      qualifiedAtOrAfter: window.startUtc,
      qualifiedBefore: window.endUtc,
    );

    return AnalyticsEntityRankings(
      period: period,
      window: window,
      songs: _rank(plays, (play) => play.songId, limit: limit),
      artists: _rank(plays, (play) => play.artistId, limit: limit),
      albums: _rank(plays, (play) => play.albumId, limit: limit),
      albumEditions: _rank(plays, (play) => play.albumEditionId, limit: limit),
    );
  }

  List<AnalyticsRankedEntity> _rank(
    List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
    String Function(QualifiedPlay play) entityIdFor, {
    required int? limit,
  }) {
    final aggregates = <String, _MutableRanking>{};

    for (final play in plays) {
      final entityId = entityIdFor(play);
      final qualifiedAt = play.qualifiedAt.toUtc();

      final existing = aggregates[entityId];

      if (existing == null) {
        aggregates[entityId] = _MutableRanking(
          entityId: entityId,
          qualifiedStreamCount: 1,
          firstQualifiedAt: qualifiedAt,
          lastQualifiedAt: qualifiedAt,
        );

        continue;
      }

      existing.qualifiedStreamCount++;

      if (qualifiedAt.isBefore(existing.firstQualifiedAt)) {
        existing.firstQualifiedAt = qualifiedAt;
      }

      if (qualifiedAt.isAfter(existing.lastQualifiedAt)) {
        existing.lastQualifiedAt = qualifiedAt;
      }
    }

    final rankings = aggregates.values
        .map(
          (entry) => AnalyticsRankedEntity(
            entityId: entry.entityId,
            qualifiedStreamCount: entry.qualifiedStreamCount,
            firstQualifiedAt: entry.firstQualifiedAt,
            lastQualifiedAt: entry.lastQualifiedAt,
          ),
        )
        .toList();

    rankings.sort((a, b) {
      final streamComparison = b.qualifiedStreamCount.compareTo(
        a.qualifiedStreamCount,
      );

      if (streamComparison != 0) {
        return streamComparison;
      }

      final recentComparison = b.lastQualifiedAt.compareTo(a.lastQualifiedAt);

      if (recentComparison != 0) {
        return recentComparison;
      }

      return a.entityId.compareTo(b.entityId);
    });

    if (limit == null || rankings.length <= limit) {
      return List<AnalyticsRankedEntity>.unmodifiable(rankings);
    }

    return List<AnalyticsRankedEntity>.unmodifiable(rankings.take(limit));
  }
}

class _MutableRanking {
  _MutableRanking({
    required this.entityId,
    required this.qualifiedStreamCount,
    required this.firstQualifiedAt,
    required this.lastQualifiedAt,
  });

  final String entityId;

  int qualifiedStreamCount;
  DateTime firstQualifiedAt;
  DateTime lastQualifiedAt;
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\analytics_listening_duration_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../domain/entities/playback_listening_segment.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_listening_segment_repository.dart';
import '../models/analytics_listening_duration.dart';
import '../models/analytics_period.dart';
import '../models/analytics_time_window.dart';
import 'analytics_time_window_resolver.dart';

class AnalyticsListeningDurationService {
  const AnalyticsListeningDurationService({
    required PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository listeningSegments,
    AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver timeWindowResolver =
        const AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver(),
  }) : _listeningSegments = listeningSegments,
       _timeWindowResolver = timeWindowResolver;

  final PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository _listeningSegments;
  final AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver _timeWindowResolver;

  Future<AnalyticsListeningDuration> load({
    required AnalyticsPeriod period,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
  }) async {
    final window = _timeWindowResolver.resolve(
      period: period,
      referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
    );

    final segments = await _listeningSegments.getAll(
      startedBefore: window.endUtc,
    );

    var totalMilliseconds = 0;

    for (final segment in segments) {
      totalMilliseconds += _durationInsideWindow(
        segment: segment,
        window: window,
      ).inMilliseconds;
    }

    return AnalyticsListeningDuration(
      period: period,
      window: window,
      totalAudibleListeningDuration: Duration(milliseconds: totalMilliseconds),
    );
  }

  Duration _durationInsideWindow({
    required PlaybackListeningSegment segment,
    required AnalyticsTimeWindow window,
  }) {
    final segmentStart = segment.startedAt.toUtc();
    final segmentEnd = _truthfulEndpoint(segment);

    if (!segmentEnd.isAfter(segmentStart)) {
      return Duration.zero;
    }

    var overlapStart = segmentStart;
    var overlapEnd = segmentEnd;

    final windowStart = window.startUtc;
    final windowEnd = window.endUtc;

    if (windowStart != null && overlapStart.isBefore(windowStart)) {
      overlapStart = windowStart;
    }

    if (windowEnd != null && overlapEnd.isAfter(windowEnd)) {
      overlapEnd = windowEnd;
    }

    if (!overlapEnd.isAfter(overlapStart)) {
      return Duration.zero;
    }

    return overlapEnd.difference(overlapStart);
  }

  DateTime _truthfulEndpoint(PlaybackListeningSegment segment) {
    final endedAt = segment.endedAt;

    if (endedAt != null) {
      return endedAt.toUtc();
    }

    final listenedDuration = segment.listenedDuration;

    if (listenedDuration <= Duration.zero) {
      return segment.startedAt.toUtc();
    }

    return segment.startedAt.toUtc().add(listenedDuration);
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\analytics_summary_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../domain/entities/qualified_play.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';
import '../models/analytics_period.dart';
import '../models/analytics_summary.dart';
import '../models/analytics_time_window.dart';
import 'analytics_time_window_resolver.dart';

class AnalyticsSummaryService {
  const AnalyticsSummaryService({
    required QualifiedPlayRepository qualifiedPlays,
    AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver timeWindowResolver =
        const AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver(),
  }) : _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays,
       _timeWindowResolver = timeWindowResolver;

  final QualifiedPlayRepository _qualifiedPlays;
  final AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver _timeWindowResolver;

  Future<AnalyticsSummary> load({
    required AnalyticsPeriod period,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
  }) async {
    final window = _timeWindowResolver.resolve(
      period: period,
      referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
    );

    final plays = await _qualifiedPlays.getAll(
      qualifiedAtOrAfter: window.startUtc,
      qualifiedBefore: window.endUtc,
    );

    return _buildSummary(period: period, window: window, plays: plays);
  }

  AnalyticsSummary _buildSummary({
    required AnalyticsPeriod period,
    required AnalyticsTimeWindow window,
    required List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
  }) {
    final songIds = <String>{};
    final artistIds = <String>{};
    final albumIds = <String>{};

    var totalListeningMilliseconds = 0;

    DateTime? firstQualifiedAt;
    DateTime? lastQualifiedAt;

    for (final play in plays) {
      songIds.add(play.songId);
      artistIds.add(play.artistId);
      albumIds.add(play.albumId);

      totalListeningMilliseconds += play.listenedDuration.inMilliseconds;

      final qualifiedAt = play.qualifiedAt.toUtc();

      if (firstQualifiedAt == null || qualifiedAt.isBefore(firstQualifiedAt)) {
        firstQualifiedAt = qualifiedAt;
      }

      if (lastQualifiedAt == null || qualifiedAt.isAfter(lastQualifiedAt)) {
        lastQualifiedAt = qualifiedAt;
      }
    }

    return AnalyticsSummary(
      period: period,
      window: window,
      qualifiedStreamCount: plays.length,
      qualifiedListeningDuration: Duration(
        milliseconds: totalListeningMilliseconds,
      ),
      distinctSongCount: songIds.length,
      distinctArtistCount: artistIds.length,
      distinctAlbumCount: albumIds.length,
      firstQualifiedAt: firstQualifiedAt,
      lastQualifiedAt: lastQualifiedAt,
    );
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\analytics_time_window_resolver.dart =====
import '../models/analytics_period.dart';
import '../models/analytics_time_window.dart';

class AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver {
  const AnalyticsTimeWindowResolver();

  AnalyticsTimeWindow resolve({
    required AnalyticsPeriod period,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
  }) {
    switch (period) {
      case AnalyticsPeriod.day:
        return _day(referenceLocal);

      case AnalyticsPeriod.week:
        return _week(referenceLocal);

      case AnalyticsPeriod.month:
        return _month(referenceLocal);

      case AnalyticsPeriod.year:
        return _year(referenceLocal);

      case AnalyticsPeriod.lifetime:
        return const AnalyticsTimeWindow(startUtc: null, endUtc: null);
    }
  }

  AnalyticsTimeWindow _day(DateTime referenceLocal) {
    final startLocal = DateTime(
      referenceLocal.year,
      referenceLocal.month,
      referenceLocal.day,
    );

    final endLocal = DateTime(
      referenceLocal.year,
      referenceLocal.month,
      referenceLocal.day + 1,
    );

    return _fromLocalBounds(startLocal, endLocal);
  }

  AnalyticsTimeWindow _week(DateTime referenceLocal) {
    final startOfDay = DateTime(
      referenceLocal.year,
      referenceLocal.month,
      referenceLocal.day,
    );

    final daysSinceSunday = startOfDay.weekday % 7;

    final startLocal = DateTime(
      startOfDay.year,
      startOfDay.month,
      startOfDay.day - daysSinceSunday,
    );

    final endLocal = DateTime(
      startLocal.year,
      startLocal.month,
      startLocal.day + 7,
    );

    return _fromLocalBounds(startLocal, endLocal);
  }

  AnalyticsTimeWindow _month(DateTime referenceLocal) {
    final startLocal = DateTime(referenceLocal.year, referenceLocal.month);

    final endLocal = DateTime(referenceLocal.year, referenceLocal.month + 1);

    return _fromLocalBounds(startLocal, endLocal);
  }

  AnalyticsTimeWindow _year(DateTime referenceLocal) {
    final startLocal = DateTime(referenceLocal.year);

    final endLocal = DateTime(referenceLocal.year + 1);

    return _fromLocalBounds(startLocal, endLocal);
  }

  AnalyticsTimeWindow _fromLocalBounds(DateTime startLocal, DateTime endLocal) {
    return AnalyticsTimeWindow(
      startUtc: startLocal.toUtc(),
      endUtc: endLocal.toUtc(),
    );
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\listening_history_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../domain/models/listening_history_cursor.dart';
import '../models/listening_history_entry.dart';
import '../models/listening_history_page.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';

class ListeningHistoryService {
  const ListeningHistoryService({
    required PlaybackSessionRepository playbackSessions,
    required QualifiedPlayRepository qualifiedPlays,
  }) : _playbackSessions = playbackSessions,
       _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays;

  final PlaybackSessionRepository _playbackSessions;
  final QualifiedPlayRepository _qualifiedPlays;

  Future<ListeningHistoryPage> getPage({
    DateTime? startedAtOrAfter,
    DateTime? startedBefore,
    ListeningHistoryCursor? before,
    int limit = 50,
  }) async {
    if (limit <= 0) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(limit, 'limit', 'Must be greater than zero.');
    }

    final sessions = await _playbackSessions.getHistoryPage(
      startedAtOrAfter: startedAtOrAfter,
      startedBefore: startedBefore,
      before: before,
      limit: limit + 1,
    );

    final hasMore = sessions.length > limit;

    final pageSessions = hasMore
        ? sessions.take(limit).toList(growable: false)
        : sessions;

    final plays = await _qualifiedPlays.getByPlaybackSessionIds(
      pageSessions.map((session) => session.id),
    );

    final playBySessionId = {
      for (final play in plays) play.playbackSessionId: play,
    };

    final entries = pageSessions
        .map(
          (session) => ListeningHistoryEntry(
            session: session,
            qualifiedPlay: playBySessionId[session.id],
          ),
        )
        .toList(growable: false);

    final nextCursor = hasMore && pageSessions.isNotEmpty
        ? ListeningHistoryCursor(
            startedAt: pageSessions.last.startedAt,
            sessionId: pageSessions.last.id,
          )
        : null;

    return ListeningHistoryPage(
      entries: entries,
      nextCursor: nextCursor,
      hasMore: hasMore,
    );
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\listening_qualification_policy.dart =====
import 'dart:math' as math;

class ListeningQualificationPolicy {
  const ListeningQualificationPolicy();

  static const Duration standardThreshold = Duration(seconds: 30);
  static const Duration shortTrackMinimum = Duration(seconds: 5);

  Duration thresholdFor(Duration trackDuration) {
    if (trackDuration <= Duration.zero) {
      return standardThreshold;
    }

    if (trackDuration >= standardThreshold) {
      return standardThreshold;
    }

    final halfDurationMilliseconds = (trackDuration.inMilliseconds / 2).ceil();

    final shortTrackThresholdMilliseconds = math.max(
      shortTrackMinimum.inMilliseconds,
      halfDurationMilliseconds,
    );

    return Duration(
      milliseconds: math.min(
        trackDuration.inMilliseconds,
        shortTrackThresholdMilliseconds,
      ),
    );
  }

  bool qualifies({
    required Duration listenedDuration,
    required Duration trackDuration,
  }) {
    if (listenedDuration.isNegative) {
      return false;
    }

    return listenedDuration >= thresholdFor(trackDuration);
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\application\services\listening_session_recovery_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../domain/entities/playback_listening_segment.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/playback_session.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_listening_segment_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';

class ListeningSessionRecoveryService {
  const ListeningSessionRecoveryService({
    required PlaybackSessionRepository playbackSessions,
    required PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository listeningSegments,
  }) : _playbackSessions = playbackSessions,
       _listeningSegments = listeningSegments;

  final PlaybackSessionRepository _playbackSessions;
  final PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository _listeningSegments;

  Future<int> recoverInterruptedSessions() async {
    final openSessions = await _playbackSessions.getOpen();

    for (final session in openSessions) {
      final openSegment = await _listeningSegments.getOpenForSession(
        session.id,
      );

      if (openSegment != null) {
        final segmentEndedAt = _safeSegmentEndpoint(openSegment);

        await _listeningSegments.save(
          openSegment.copyWith(
            endedAt: segmentEndedAt,
            updatedAt: segmentEndedAt,
          ),
        );
      }

      final segments = await _listeningSegments.getForSession(session.id);

      final recoveredListeningDuration = segments.fold<Duration>(
        Duration.zero,
        (total, segment) => total + segment.listenedDuration,
      );

      final endedAt = segments.isEmpty
          ? _safeSessionEndpoint(session)
          : _latestSegmentEndpoint(segments);

      await _playbackSessions.save(
        session.copyWith(
          endedAt: endedAt,
          listenedDuration: recoveredListeningDuration,
          endReason: PlaybackSessionEndReason.interrupted,
          updatedAt: endedAt,
        ),
      );
    }

    return openSessions.length;
  }

  DateTime _safeSessionEndpoint(PlaybackSession session) {
    return session.updatedAt.isBefore(session.startedAt)
        ? session.startedAt
        : session.updatedAt;
  }

  DateTime _latestSegmentEndpoint(List<PlaybackListeningSegment> segments) {
    var latest = _safeSegmentEndpoint(segments.first);

    for (final segment in segments.skip(1)) {
      final endpoint = _safeSegmentEndpoint(segment);

      if (endpoint.isAfter(latest)) {
        latest = endpoint;
      }
    }

    return latest;
  }

  DateTime _safeSegmentEndpoint(PlaybackListeningSegment segment) {
    final endpoint =
        segment.endedAt ?? segment.startedAt.add(segment.listenedDuration);

    return endpoint.isBefore(segment.startedAt) ? segment.startedAt : endpoint;
  }
}

// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../../../core/identity/entity_id.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/playback_listening_segment.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/playback_session.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/qualified_play.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_listening_segment_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';
import 'listening_qualification_policy.dart';
import 'listening_session_mutation_runner.dart';
import 'listening_session_start.dart';

typedef AnalyticsNow = DateTime Function();
typedef AnalyticsIdGenerator = String Function();

class ListeningSessionTracker {
  ListeningSessionTracker({
    required PlaybackSessionRepository playbackSessions,
    required QualifiedPlayRepository qualifiedPlays,
    required PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository listeningSegments,
    required ListeningSessionMutationRunner mutationRunner,
    ListeningQualificationPolicy qualificationPolicy =
        const ListeningQualificationPolicy(),
    AnalyticsNow? now,
    AnalyticsIdGenerator? playbackSessionIdGenerator,
    AnalyticsIdGenerator? listeningSegmentIdGenerator,
    AnalyticsIdGenerator? qualifiedPlayIdGenerator,
  }) : _playbackSessions = playbackSessions,
       _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays,
       _listeningSegments = listeningSegments,
       _mutationRunner = mutationRunner,
       _qualificationPolicy = qualificationPolicy,
       _now = now ?? DateTime.now,
       _playbackSessionIdGenerator =
           playbackSessionIdGenerator ?? EntityId.playbackSession,
       _listeningSegmentIdGenerator =
           listeningSegmentIdGenerator ?? EntityId.listeningSegment,
       _qualifiedPlayIdGenerator =
           qualifiedPlayIdGenerator ?? EntityId.qualifiedPlay;

  final PlaybackSessionRepository _playbackSessions;
  final QualifiedPlayRepository _qualifiedPlays;
  final PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository _listeningSegments;
  final ListeningSessionMutationRunner _mutationRunner;
  final ListeningQualificationPolicy _qualificationPolicy;

  final AnalyticsNow _now;
  final AnalyticsIdGenerator _playbackSessionIdGenerator;
  final AnalyticsIdGenerator _listeningSegmentIdGenerator;
  final AnalyticsIdGenerator _qualifiedPlayIdGenerator;

  PlaybackSession? _session;
  PlaybackListeningSegment? _listeningSegment;
  DateTime? _playingSince;
  bool _hasQualified = false;

  PlaybackSession? get currentSession => _session;

  PlaybackListeningSegment? get currentListeningSegment => _listeningSegment;

  bool get hasActiveSession => _session != null;

  bool get isPlaying => _playingSince != null;

  bool get hasQualified => _hasQualified;

  Future<void> attachCurrentSessionToUser(String userId) async {
    final normalizedUserId = userId.trim();

    if (normalizedUserId.isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(userId, 'userId', 'User id must not be blank.');
    }

    final current = _session;

    if (current == null) {
      return;
    }

    final currentOwner = current.userId;

    if (currentOwner != null && currentOwner != normalizedUserId) {
      throw StateError(
        'Active playback session ${current.id} belongs to a different user.',
      );
    }

    final existingPlay = await _qualifiedPlays.getByPlaybackSessionId(
      current.id,
    );

    final playOwner = existingPlay?.userId;

    if (playOwner != null && playOwner != normalizedUserId) {
      throw StateError(
        'Qualified play ${existingPlay!.id} belongs to a different user.',
      );
    }

    if (existingPlay != null && existingPlay.userId == null) {
      await _qualifiedPlays.save(
        existingPlay.copyWith(userId: normalizedUserId),
      );
    }

    if (currentOwner == null) {
      final attached = current.copyWith(userId: normalizedUserId);

      await _playbackSessions.save(attached);

      _session = attached;
    }
  }

  Future<PlaybackSession> start(
    ListeningSessionStart start, {
    Duration initialPosition = Duration.zero,
    bool playing = false,
  }) async {
    if (_session != null) {
      throw StateError(
        'Cannot start a listening session while another session is active.',
      );
    }

    final now = _utcNow();

    final session = PlaybackSession(
      id: _playbackSessionIdGenerator(),
      userId: start.userId,
      songId: start.songId,
      artistId: start.artistId,
      albumId: start.albumId,
      albumEditionId: start.albumEditionId,
      songTitle: start.songTitle,
      artistName: start.artistName,
      albumTitle: start.albumTitle,
      albumEditionTitle: start.albumEditionTitle,
      startedAt: now,
      listenedDuration: Duration.zero,
      trackDuration: start.trackDuration,
      finalPosition: initialPosition,
      sourceContext: start.sourceContext,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
    );

    await _playbackSessions.create(session);

    _session = session;
    _listeningSegment = null;
    _hasQualified = false;

    if (playing) {
      await _startListeningSegment(now);
      _playingSince = now;
    } else {
      _playingSince = null;
    }

    await _qualifyIfNeeded(now);

    return _session!;
  }

  Future<void> markPlaying({
    Duration? position,
    Duration? trackDuration,
  }) async {
    final current = _requireSession();

    if (_playingSince != null) {
      if (position != null || trackDuration != null) {
        await checkpoint(
          position: position ?? current.finalPosition,
          trackDuration: trackDuration,
        );
      }
      return;
    }

    final now = _utcNow();

    final updated = current.copyWith(
      finalPosition: position ?? current.finalPosition,
      trackDuration: _resolvedTrackDuration(
        current: current.trackDuration,
        incoming: trackDuration,
      ),
      updatedAt: now,
    );

    await _playbackSessions.save(updated);

    _session = updated;

    await _startListeningSegment(now);

    _playingSince = now;

    await _qualifyIfNeeded(now);
  }

  Future<void> markPaused({Duration? position, Duration? trackDuration}) async {
    final current = _requireSession();
    final now = _utcNow();

    final updated = _withAccumulatedListening(
      current,
      now: now,
      position: position ?? current.finalPosition,
      trackDuration: trackDuration,
    );

    final updatedSegment = _playingSince != null
        ? _updatedListeningSegment(now, close: true)
        : null;

    if (updatedSegment != null) {
      await _mutationRunner.run((context) async {
        await context.listeningSegments.save(updatedSegment);
        await context.playbackSessions.save(updated);
      });
    } else {
      await _playbackSessions.save(updated);
    }

    _session = updated;

    if (updatedSegment != null) {
      _listeningSegment = updatedSegment;
    }

    _playingSince = null;

    await _qualifyIfNeeded(now);
  }

  Future<void> checkpoint({
    required Duration position,
    Duration? trackDuration,
  }) async {
    final current = _requireSession();
    final now = _utcNow();

    final updated = _withAccumulatedListening(
      current,
      now: now,
      position: position,
      trackDuration: trackDuration,
    );

    final updatedSegment = _playingSince != null
        ? _updatedListeningSegment(now, close: false)
        : null;

    if (updatedSegment != null) {
      await _mutationRunner.run((context) async {
        await context.listeningSegments.save(updatedSegment);
        await context.playbackSessions.save(updated);
      });
    } else {
      await _playbackSessions.save(updated);
    }

    _session = updated;

    if (updatedSegment != null) {
      _listeningSegment = updatedSegment;
      _playingSince = now;
    }

    await _qualifyIfNeeded(now);
  }

  Future<PlaybackSession> end({
    required PlaybackSessionEndReason reason,
    required Duration position,
    Duration? trackDuration,
  }) async {
    final current = _requireSession();
    final now = _utcNow();

    final accumulated = _withAccumulatedListening(
      current,
      now: now,
      position: position,
      trackDuration: trackDuration,
    );

    final ended = accumulated.copyWith(
      endedAt: now,
      endReason: reason,
      updatedAt: now,
    );

    final updatedSegment = _playingSince != null
        ? _updatedListeningSegment(now, close: true)
        : null;

    if (updatedSegment != null) {
      await _mutationRunner.run((context) async {
        await context.listeningSegments.save(updatedSegment);
        await context.playbackSessions.save(ended);
      });
    } else {
      await _playbackSessions.save(ended);
    }

    _session = ended;

    if (updatedSegment != null) {
      _listeningSegment = updatedSegment;
    }

    await _qualifyIfNeeded(now);

    _session = null;
    _listeningSegment = null;
    _playingSince = null;
    _hasQualified = false;

    return ended;
  }

  PlaybackSession _withAccumulatedListening(
    PlaybackSession session, {
    required DateTime now,
    required Duration position,
    Duration? trackDuration,
  }) {
    var listenedDuration = session.listenedDuration;

    final playingSince = _playingSince;

    if (playingSince != null && now.isAfter(playingSince)) {
      listenedDuration += now.difference(playingSince);
    }

    return session.copyWith(
      listenedDuration: listenedDuration,
      trackDuration: _resolvedTrackDuration(
        current: session.trackDuration,
        incoming: trackDuration,
      ),
      finalPosition: position,
      updatedAt: now,
    );
  }

  Future<void> _startListeningSegment(DateTime startedAt) async {
    final existing = _listeningSegment;

    if (existing != null && !existing.isEnded) {
      throw StateError(
        'Cannot start a listening segment while another segment is active.',
      );
    }

    final session = _requireSession();

    final segment = PlaybackListeningSegment(
      id: _listeningSegmentIdGenerator(),
      playbackSessionId: session.id,
      startedAt: startedAt,
      listenedDuration: Duration.zero,
      createdAt: startedAt,
      updatedAt: startedAt,
    );

    await _listeningSegments.create(segment);

    _listeningSegment = segment;
  }

  PlaybackListeningSegment _updatedListeningSegment(
    DateTime now, {
    required bool close,
  }) {
    final current = _listeningSegment;

    if (current == null || current.isEnded) {
      throw StateError('Audible playback has no active listening segment.');
    }

    final listenedDuration = now.isAfter(current.startedAt)
        ? now.difference(current.startedAt)
        : Duration.zero;

    return current.copyWith(
      endedAt: close ? now : null,
      listenedDuration: listenedDuration,
      updatedAt: now,
    );
  }

  Duration _resolvedTrackDuration({
    required Duration current,
    required Duration? incoming,
  }) {
    if (incoming == null || incoming <= Duration.zero) {
      return current;
    }

    return incoming;
  }

  Future<void> _qualifyIfNeeded(DateTime qualifiedAt) async {
    final session = _requireSession();

    if (_hasQualified) {
      return;
    }

    final existing = await _qualifiedPlays.getByPlaybackSessionId(session.id);

    if (existing != null) {
      _hasQualified = true;
      return;
    }

    if (!_qualificationPolicy.qualifies(
      listenedDuration: session.listenedDuration,
      trackDuration: session.trackDuration,
    )) {
      return;
    }

    final play = QualifiedPlay(
      id: _qualifiedPlayIdGenerator(),
      playbackSessionId: session.id,
      userId: session.userId,
      songId: session.songId,
      artistId: session.artistId,
      albumId: session.albumId,
      albumEditionId: session.albumEditionId,
      qualifiedAt: qualifiedAt,
      listenedDuration: session.listenedDuration,
      trackDuration: session.trackDuration,
      createdAt: qualifiedAt,
    );

    await _qualifiedPlays.create(play);

    _hasQualified = true;
  }

  PlaybackSession _requireSession() {
    final current = _session;

    if (current == null) {
      throw StateError('No active listening session.');
    }

    return current;
  }

  DateTime _utcNow() => _now().toUtc();
}

===== lib\features\analytics\data\daos\playback_listening_segment_dao.dart =====
import 'package:sqflite/sqflite.dart';

import '../../../../core/database/database_tables.dart';

class PlaybackListeningSegmentDao {
  const PlaybackListeningSegmentDao(this.db);

  final DatabaseExecutor db;

  Future<void> insert(Map<String, Object?> row) async {
    await db.insert(DatabaseTables.playbackListeningSegments, row);
  }

  Future<void> update(Map<String, Object?> row) async {
    final id = row['id'] as String;

    final updated = await db.update(
      DatabaseTables.playbackListeningSegments,
      row,
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
    );

    if (updated == 0) {
      throw StateError('Playback listening segment not found: $id');
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, Object?>?> findById(String id) async {
    final rows = await db.query(
      DatabaseTables.playbackListeningSegments,
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
      limit: 1,
    );

    return rows.isEmpty ? null : rows.single;
  }

  Future<Map<String, Object?>?> findOpenForSession(
    String playbackSessionId,
  ) async {
    final rows = await db.query(
      DatabaseTables.playbackListeningSegments,
      where: 'playback_session_id = ? AND ended_at IS NULL',
      whereArgs: [playbackSessionId],
      orderBy: 'started_at DESC, id DESC',
      limit: 1,
    );

    return rows.isEmpty ? null : rows.single;
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, Object?>>> findForSession(String playbackSessionId) {
    return db.query(
      DatabaseTables.playbackListeningSegments,
      where: 'playback_session_id = ?',
      whereArgs: [playbackSessionId],
      orderBy: 'started_at ASC, id ASC',
    );
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, Object?>>> findAll({DateTime? startedBefore}) async {
    return db.query(
      DatabaseTables.playbackListeningSegments,
      where: startedBefore == null ? null : 'started_at < ?',
      whereArgs: startedBefore == null
          ? null
          : [startedBefore.toUtc().toIso8601String()],
      orderBy: 'started_at ASC, id ASC',
    );
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\data\daos\playback_session_dao.dart =====
import 'package:sqflite/sqflite.dart';

import '../../../../core/database/database_tables.dart';

class PlaybackSessionDao {
  const PlaybackSessionDao(this.db);

  final DatabaseExecutor db;

  Future<void> insert(Map<String, Object?> row) async {
    await db.insert(DatabaseTables.playbackSessions, row);
  }

  Future<void> update(Map<String, Object?> row) async {
    final id = row['id'] as String;

    final updated = await db.update(
      DatabaseTables.playbackSessions,
      row,
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
    );

    if (updated == 0) {
      throw StateError('Playback session not found: $id');
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, Object?>?> findById(String id) async {
    final rows = await db.query(
      DatabaseTables.playbackSessions,
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
      limit: 1,
    );

    return rows.isEmpty ? null : rows.single;
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, Object?>>> findByIds(Iterable<String> ids) async {
    final uniqueIds = ids.toSet().toList(growable: false);

    if (uniqueIds.isEmpty) {
      return const [];
    }

    final placeholders = List.filled(uniqueIds.length, '?').join(', ');

    return db.query(
      DatabaseTables.playbackSessions,
      where: 'id IN ($placeholders)',
      whereArgs: uniqueIds,
      orderBy: 'id ASC',
    );
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, Object?>>> findOpen() {
    return db.query(
      DatabaseTables.playbackSessions,
      where: 'ended_at IS NULL',
      orderBy: 'started_at ASC, id ASC',
    );
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, Object?>>> findAll({
    DateTime? startedAtOrAfter,
    DateTime? startedBefore,
  }) async {
    final clauses = <String>[];
    final args = <Object?>[];

    if (startedAtOrAfter != null) {
      clauses.add('started_at >= ?');
      args.add(startedAtOrAfter.toUtc().toIso8601String());
    }

    if (startedBefore != null) {
      clauses.add('started_at < ?');
      args.add(startedBefore.toUtc().toIso8601String());
    }

    return db.query(
      DatabaseTables.playbackSessions,
      where: clauses.isEmpty ? null : clauses.join(' AND '),
      whereArgs: args.isEmpty ? null : args,
      orderBy: 'started_at ASC, id ASC',
    );
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, Object?>>> findHistoryPage({
    DateTime? startedAtOrAfter,
    DateTime? startedBefore,
    DateTime? cursorStartedAt,
    String? cursorSessionId,
    required int limit,
  }) async {
    if (limit <= 0) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(limit, 'limit', 'Must be greater than zero.');
    }

    if ((cursorStartedAt == null) != (cursorSessionId == null)) {
      throw ArgumentError(
        'History cursor requires both startedAt and sessionId.',
      );
    }

    final clauses = <String>[];
    final args = <Object?>[];

    if (startedAtOrAfter != null) {
      clauses.add('started_at >= ?');
      args.add(startedAtOrAfter.toUtc().toIso8601String());
    }

    if (startedBefore != null) {
      clauses.add('started_at < ?');
      args.add(startedBefore.toUtc().toIso8601String());
    }

    if (cursorStartedAt != null && cursorSessionId != null) {
      final cursorTime = cursorStartedAt.toUtc().toIso8601String();

      clauses.add('(started_at < ? OR (started_at = ? AND id < ?))');

      args.add(cursorTime);
      args.add(cursorTime);
      args.add(cursorSessionId);
    }

    return db.query(
      DatabaseTables.playbackSessions,
      where: clauses.isEmpty ? null : clauses.join(' AND '),
      whereArgs: args.isEmpty ? null : args,
      orderBy: 'started_at DESC, id DESC',
      limit: limit,
    );
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\data\daos\qualified_play_dao.dart =====
import 'package:sqflite/sqflite.dart';

import '../../../../core/database/database_tables.dart';

class QualifiedPlayDao {
  const QualifiedPlayDao(this.db);

  final DatabaseExecutor db;

  Future<void> insert(Map<String, Object?> row) async {
    await db.insert(DatabaseTables.qualifiedPlays, row);
  }

  Future<void> update(Map<String, Object?> row) async {
    final id = row['id'];

    if (id is! String || id.trim().isEmpty) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        id,
        'id',
        'Qualified play id must not be blank.',
      );
    }

    final updated = await db.update(
      DatabaseTables.qualifiedPlays,
      row,
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
    );

    if (updated != 1) {
      throw StateError('Qualified play not found: $id');
    }
  }

  Future<Map<String, Object?>?> findById(String id) async {
    final rows = await db.query(
      DatabaseTables.qualifiedPlays,
      where: 'id = ?',
      whereArgs: [id],
      limit: 1,
    );

    return rows.isEmpty ? null : rows.single;
  }

  Future<Map<String, Object?>?> findByPlaybackSessionId(
    String playbackSessionId,
  ) async {
    final rows = await db.query(
      DatabaseTables.qualifiedPlays,
      where: 'playback_session_id = ?',
      whereArgs: [playbackSessionId],
      limit: 1,
    );

    return rows.isEmpty ? null : rows.single;
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, Object?>>> findByPlaybackSessionIds(
    Iterable<String> playbackSessionIds,
  ) async {
    final ids = playbackSessionIds.toSet().toList(growable: false);

    if (ids.isEmpty) {
      return const [];
    }

    final placeholders = List.filled(ids.length, '?').join(', ');

    return db.query(
      DatabaseTables.qualifiedPlays,
      where: 'playback_session_id IN ($placeholders)',
      whereArgs: ids,
      orderBy: 'playback_session_id ASC',
    );
  }

  Future<List<Map<String, Object?>>> findAll({
    DateTime? qualifiedAtOrAfter,
    DateTime? qualifiedBefore,
  }) async {
    final clauses = <String>[];
    final args = <Object?>[];

    if (qualifiedAtOrAfter != null) {
      clauses.add('qualified_at >= ?');
      args.add(qualifiedAtOrAfter.toUtc().toIso8601String());
    }

    if (qualifiedBefore != null) {
      clauses.add('qualified_at < ?');
      args.add(qualifiedBefore.toUtc().toIso8601String());
    }

    return db.query(
      DatabaseTables.qualifiedPlays,
      where: clauses.isEmpty ? null : clauses.join(' AND '),
      whereArgs: args.isEmpty ? null : args,
      orderBy: 'qualified_at ASC, id ASC',
    );
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\data\mappers\playback_listening_segment_row_mapper.dart =====
import '../../domain/entities/playback_listening_segment.dart';

abstract final class PlaybackListeningSegmentRowMapper {
  static PlaybackListeningSegment fromRow(Map<String, Object?> row) {
    return PlaybackListeningSegment(
      id: row['id']! as String,
      playbackSessionId: row['playback_session_id']! as String,
      startedAt: DateTime.parse(row['started_at']! as String),
      endedAt: row['ended_at'] == null
          ? null
          : DateTime.parse(row['ended_at']! as String),
      listenedDuration: Duration(
        milliseconds: row['listened_duration_ms']! as int,
      ),
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(row['created_at']! as String),
      updatedAt: DateTime.parse(row['updated_at']! as String),
    );
  }

  static Map<String, Object?> toRow(PlaybackListeningSegment segment) {
    return <String, Object?>{
      'id': segment.id,
      'playback_session_id': segment.playbackSessionId,
      'started_at': segment.startedAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      'ended_at': segment.endedAt?.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      'listened_duration_ms': segment.listenedDuration.inMilliseconds,
      'created_at': segment.createdAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      'updated_at': segment.updatedAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
    };
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\data\mappers\playback_session_row_mapper.dart =====
import '../../domain/entities/playback_session.dart';

abstract final class PlaybackSessionRowMapper {
  static PlaybackSession fromRow(Map<String, Object?> row) {
    return PlaybackSession(
      id: row['id']! as String,
      userId: row['user_id'] as String?,
      songId: row['song_id']! as String,
      artistId: row['artist_id']! as String,
      albumId: row['album_id']! as String,
      albumEditionId: row['album_edition_id']! as String,
      songTitle: row['song_title']! as String,
      artistName: row['artist_name']! as String,
      albumTitle: row['album_title']! as String,
      albumEditionTitle: row['album_edition_title']! as String,
      startedAt: DateTime.parse(row['started_at']! as String),
      endedAt: row['ended_at'] == null
          ? null
          : DateTime.parse(row['ended_at']! as String),
      listenedDuration: Duration(
        milliseconds: row['listened_duration_ms']! as int,
      ),
      trackDuration: Duration(milliseconds: row['track_duration_ms']! as int),
      finalPosition: Duration(milliseconds: row['final_position_ms']! as int),
      endReason: row['end_reason'] == null
          ? null
          : PlaybackSessionEndReason.values.firstWhere(
              (value) => value.name == row['end_reason'],
            ),
      sourceContext: row['source_context'] as String?,
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(row['created_at']! as String),
      updatedAt: DateTime.parse(row['updated_at']! as String),
    );
  }

  static Map<String, Object?> toRow(PlaybackSession session) {
    return <String, Object?>{
      'id': session.id,
      'user_id': session.userId,
      'song_id': session.songId,
      'artist_id': session.artistId,
      'album_id': session.albumId,
      'album_edition_id': session.albumEditionId,
      'song_title': session.songTitle,
      'artist_name': session.artistName,
      'album_title': session.albumTitle,
      'album_edition_title': session.albumEditionTitle,
      'started_at': session.startedAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      'ended_at': session.endedAt?.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      'listened_duration_ms': session.listenedDuration.inMilliseconds,
      'track_duration_ms': session.trackDuration.inMilliseconds,
      'final_position_ms': session.finalPosition.inMilliseconds,
      'end_reason': session.endReason?.name,
      'source_context': session.sourceContext,
      'created_at': session.createdAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      'updated_at': session.updatedAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
    };
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\data\mappers\qualified_play_row_mapper.dart =====
import '../../domain/entities/qualified_play.dart';

abstract final class QualifiedPlayRowMapper {
  static QualifiedPlay fromRow(Map<String, Object?> row) {
    return QualifiedPlay(
      id: row['id']! as String,
      playbackSessionId: row['playback_session_id']! as String,
      userId: row['user_id'] as String?,
      songId: row['song_id']! as String,
      artistId: row['artist_id']! as String,
      albumId: row['album_id']! as String,
      albumEditionId: row['album_edition_id']! as String,
      qualifiedAt: DateTime.parse(row['qualified_at']! as String),
      listenedDuration: Duration(
        milliseconds: row['listened_duration_ms']! as int,
      ),
      trackDuration: Duration(milliseconds: row['track_duration_ms']! as int),
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(row['created_at']! as String),
    );
  }

  static Map<String, Object?> toRow(QualifiedPlay play) {
    return <String, Object?>{
      'id': play.id,
      'playback_session_id': play.playbackSessionId,
      'user_id': play.userId,
      'song_id': play.songId,
      'artist_id': play.artistId,
      'album_id': play.albumId,
      'album_edition_id': play.albumEditionId,
      'qualified_at': play.qualifiedAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      'listened_duration_ms': play.listenedDuration.inMilliseconds,
      'track_duration_ms': play.trackDuration.inMilliseconds,
      'created_at': play.createdAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
    };
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\data\repositories\listening_session_mutation_transaction_runner.dart =====
import '../../../../core/database/app_database.dart';
import '../../application/services/listening_session_mutation_context.dart';
import '../../application/services/listening_session_mutation_runner.dart';
import '../daos/playback_listening_segment_dao.dart';
import '../daos/playback_session_dao.dart';
import '../daos/qualified_play_dao.dart';
import 'sqlite_playback_listening_segment_repository.dart';
import 'sqlite_playback_session_repository.dart';
import 'sqlite_qualified_play_repository.dart';

class ListeningSessionMutationTransactionRunner
    implements ListeningSessionMutationRunner {
  const ListeningSessionMutationTransactionRunner({required this.appDatabase});

  final AppDatabase appDatabase;

  @override
  Future<T> run<T>(ListeningSessionMutationAction<T> action) {
    return appDatabase.transaction((transaction) async {
      final context = ListeningSessionMutationContext(
        playbackSessions: SqlitePlaybackSessionRepository(
          PlaybackSessionDao(transaction),
        ),
        listeningSegments: SqlitePlaybackListeningSegmentRepository(
          PlaybackListeningSegmentDao(transaction),
        ),
        qualifiedPlays: SqliteQualifiedPlayRepository(
          QualifiedPlayDao(transaction),
        ),
      );

      return action(context);
    });
  }
}

  Future<void> create(PlaybackSession session) {
    return dao.insert(PlaybackSessionRowMapper.toRow(session));
  }

  @override
  Future<void> save(PlaybackSession session) {
    return dao.update(PlaybackSessionRowMapper.toRow(session));
  }

  @override
  Future<PlaybackSession?> getById(String id) async {
    final row = await dao.findById(id);

    return row == null ? null : PlaybackSessionRowMapper.fromRow(row);
  }

  @override
  Future<List<PlaybackSession>> getByIds(Iterable<String> ids) async {
    final rows = await dao.findByIds(ids);

    return rows.map(PlaybackSessionRowMapper.fromRow).toList(growable: false);
  }

  @override
  Future<List<PlaybackSession>> getOpen() async {
    final rows = await dao.findOpen();

    return rows.map(PlaybackSessionRowMapper.fromRow).toList(growable: false);
  }

  @override
  Future<List<PlaybackSession>> getAll({
    DateTime? startedAtOrAfter,
    DateTime? startedBefore,
  }) async {
    final rows = await dao.findAll(
      startedAtOrAfter: startedAtOrAfter,
      startedBefore: startedBefore,
    );

    return rows.map(PlaybackSessionRowMapper.fromRow).toList(growable: false);
  }

  @override
  Future<List<PlaybackSession>> getHistoryPage({
    DateTime? startedAtOrAfter,
    DateTime? startedBefore,
    ListeningHistoryCursor? before,
    required int limit,
  }) async {
    final rows = await dao.findHistoryPage(
      startedAtOrAfter: startedAtOrAfter,
      startedBefore: startedBefore,
      cursorStartedAt: before?.startedAt,
      cursorSessionId: before?.sessionId,
      limit: limit,
    );

    return rows.map(PlaybackSessionRowMapper.fromRow).toList(growable: false);
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\domain\entities\playback_listening_segment.dart =====
class PlaybackListeningSegment {
  static const Object _unset = Object();

  const PlaybackListeningSegment({
    required this.id,
    required this.playbackSessionId,
    required this.startedAt,
    required this.listenedDuration,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.endedAt,
  });

  final String id;
  final String playbackSessionId;

  final DateTime startedAt;
  final DateTime? endedAt;

  final Duration listenedDuration;

  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  bool get isEnded => endedAt != null;

  PlaybackListeningSegment copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? playbackSessionId,
    DateTime? startedAt,
    Object? endedAt = _unset,
    Duration? listenedDuration,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return PlaybackListeningSegment(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      playbackSessionId: playbackSessionId ?? this.playbackSessionId,
      startedAt: startedAt ?? this.startedAt,
      endedAt: identical(endedAt, _unset) ? this.endedAt : endedAt as DateTime?,
      listenedDuration: listenedDuration ?? this.listenedDuration,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) {
    return identical(this, other) ||
        other is PlaybackListeningSegment && other.id == id;
  }

  @override
  int get hashCode => id.hashCode;
}

===== lib\features\analytics\domain\entities\playback_session.dart =====
enum PlaybackSessionEndReason {
  completed,
  skipped,
  stopped,
  replaced,
  interrupted,
  failed,
}

class PlaybackSession {
  static const Object _unset = Object();

  const PlaybackSession({
    required this.id,
    required this.songId,
    required this.artistId,
    required this.albumId,
    required this.albumEditionId,
    required this.songTitle,
    required this.artistName,
    required this.albumTitle,
    required this.albumEditionTitle,
    required this.startedAt,
    required this.listenedDuration,
    required this.trackDuration,
    required this.finalPosition,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.userId,
    this.endedAt,
    this.endReason,
    this.sourceContext,
  });

  final String id;
  final String? userId;

  final String songId;
  final String artistId;
  final String albumId;
  final String albumEditionId;

  final String songTitle;
  final String artistName;
  final String albumTitle;
  final String albumEditionTitle;

  final DateTime startedAt;
  final DateTime? endedAt;

  final Duration listenedDuration;
  final Duration trackDuration;
  final Duration finalPosition;

  final PlaybackSessionEndReason? endReason;
  final String? sourceContext;

  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  bool get isEnded => endedAt != null;

  PlaybackSession copyWith({
    String? id,
    Object? userId = _unset,
    String? songId,
    String? artistId,
    String? albumId,
    String? albumEditionId,
    String? songTitle,
    String? artistName,
    String? albumTitle,
    String? albumEditionTitle,
    DateTime? startedAt,
    Object? endedAt = _unset,
    Duration? listenedDuration,
    Duration? trackDuration,
    Duration? finalPosition,
    Object? endReason = _unset,
    Object? sourceContext = _unset,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return PlaybackSession(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      userId: identical(userId, _unset) ? this.userId : userId as String?,
      songId: songId ?? this.songId,
      artistId: artistId ?? this.artistId,
      albumId: albumId ?? this.albumId,
      albumEditionId: albumEditionId ?? this.albumEditionId,
      songTitle: songTitle ?? this.songTitle,
      artistName: artistName ?? this.artistName,
      albumTitle: albumTitle ?? this.albumTitle,
      albumEditionTitle: albumEditionTitle ?? this.albumEditionTitle,
      startedAt: startedAt ?? this.startedAt,
      endedAt: identical(endedAt, _unset) ? this.endedAt : endedAt as DateTime?,
      listenedDuration: listenedDuration ?? this.listenedDuration,
      trackDuration: trackDuration ?? this.trackDuration,
      finalPosition: finalPosition ?? this.finalPosition,
      endReason: identical(endReason, _unset)
          ? this.endReason
          : endReason as PlaybackSessionEndReason?,
      sourceContext: identical(sourceContext, _unset)
          ? this.sourceContext
          : sourceContext as String?,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object other) {
    return identical(this, other) || other is PlaybackSession && other.id == id;
  }

  @override
  int get hashCode => id.hashCode;
}
