===== lib\features\analytics\presentation\controllers\listening_history_controller.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../application/models/listening_history_entry.dart';
import '../../application/services/listening_history_service.dart';
import '../../domain/models/listening_history_cursor.dart';

class ListeningHistoryController extends ChangeNotifier {
  ListeningHistoryController({required this._service, int pageSize = 50})
    : _pageSize = _validatedPageSize(pageSize);

  final ListeningHistoryService _service;
  final int _pageSize;

  List<ListeningHistoryEntry> _entries = const [];
  ListeningHistoryCursor? _nextCursor;

  DateTime? _startedAtOrAfter;
  DateTime? _startedBefore;

  bool _isLoading = false;
  bool _isLoadingMore = false;
  bool _hasMore = false;
  Object? _error;

  int _requestGeneration = 0;

  List<ListeningHistoryEntry> get entries => _entries;

  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;

  bool get isLoadingMore => _isLoadingMore;

  bool get hasMore => _hasMore;

  Object? get error => _error;

  bool get hasError => _error != null;

  bool get isEmpty =>
      !_isLoading && !_isLoadingMore && _error == null && _entries.isEmpty;

  DateTime? get startedAtOrAfter => _startedAtOrAfter;

  DateTime? get startedBefore => _startedBefore;

  bool get hasDateFilter => _startedAtOrAfter != null || _startedBefore != null;

  Future<void> load() {
    if (_isLoading) {
      return Future.value();
    }

    return _loadFirstPage();
  }

  Future<void> refresh() {
    return _loadFirstPage();
  }

  Future<void> setDateRange({
    DateTime? startedAtOrAfter,
    DateTime? startedBefore,
  }) async {
    if (startedAtOrAfter != null &&
        startedBefore != null &&
        !startedAtOrAfter.isBefore(startedBefore)) {
      throw ArgumentError(
        'startedAtOrAfter must be earlier than startedBefore.',
      );
    }

    _startedAtOrAfter = startedAtOrAfter;
    _startedBefore = startedBefore;

    await _loadFirstPage();
  }

  Future<void> clearDateRange() {
    return setDateRange();
  }

  Future<void> loadMore() async {
    if (_isLoading || _isLoadingMore || !_hasMore || _nextCursor == null) {
      return;
    }

    final generation = _requestGeneration;
    final cursor = _nextCursor;

    _isLoadingMore = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final page = await _service.getPage(
        startedAtOrAfter: _startedAtOrAfter,
        startedBefore: _startedBefore,
        before: cursor,
        limit: _pageSize,
      );

      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _entries = List<ListeningHistoryEntry>.unmodifiable([
        ..._entries,
        ...page.entries,
      ]);

      _nextCursor = page.nextCursor;
      _hasMore = page.hasMore;
    } catch (error) {
      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _error = error;
    } finally {
      if (generation == _requestGeneration) {
        _isLoadingMore = false;
        notifyListeners();
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadFirstPage() async {
    final generation = ++_requestGeneration;

    _isLoading = true;
    _isLoadingMore = false;
    _error = null;

    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final page = await _service.getPage(
        startedAtOrAfter: _startedAtOrAfter,
        startedBefore: _startedBefore,
        limit: _pageSize,
      );

      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _entries = List<ListeningHistoryEntry>.unmodifiable(page.entries);
      _nextCursor = page.nextCursor;
      _hasMore = page.hasMore;
    } catch (error) {
      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _error = error;
    } finally {
      if (generation == _requestGeneration) {
        _isLoading = false;
        notifyListeners();
      }
    }
  }

  static int _validatedPageSize(int pageSize) {
    if (pageSize <= 0) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        pageSize,
        'pageSize',
        'Must be greater than zero.',
      );
    }

    return pageSize;
  }
}

===== lib\features\analytics\presentation\pages\listening_history_page.dart =====
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../../application/models/listening_history_entry.dart';
import '../../application/services/listening_history_service.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/playback_session.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';
import '../controllers/listening_history_controller.dart';

class ListeningHistoryPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const ListeningHistoryPage({super.key, this.controller});

  final ListeningHistoryController? controller;

  @override
  State<ListeningHistoryPage> createState() => _ListeningHistoryPageState();
}

class _ListeningHistoryPageState extends State<ListeningHistoryPage> {
  ListeningHistoryController? _controller;

  PlaybackSessionRepository? _playbackSessions;
  QualifiedPlayRepository? _qualifiedPlays;

  bool _ownsController = false;

  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();

    final suppliedController = widget.controller;

    if (suppliedController != null) {
      if (!identical(_controller, suppliedController)) {
        _disposeOwnedController();

        _controller = suppliedController;
        _ownsController = false;
      }

      return;
    }

    final playbackSessions = context.watch<PlaybackSessionRepository?>();
    final qualifiedPlays = context.watch<QualifiedPlayRepository?>();

    if (playbackSessions == null || qualifiedPlays == null) {
      _playbackSessions = playbackSessions;
      _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays;

      _disposeOwnedController();
      _controller = null;

      return;
    }

    if (identical(playbackSessions, _playbackSessions) &&
        identical(qualifiedPlays, _qualifiedPlays) &&
        _controller != null) {
      return;
    }

    _playbackSessions = playbackSessions;
    _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays;

    _disposeOwnedController();

    final controller = ListeningHistoryController(
      service: ListeningHistoryService(
        playbackSessions: playbackSessions,
        qualifiedPlays: qualifiedPlays,
      ),
    );

    _controller = controller;
    _ownsController = true;

    unawaited(controller.load());
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final controller = _controller;

    return Scaffold(
      key: const ValueKey<String>('listening-history-page'),
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Listening history'),
        actions: [
          if (controller != null)
            ListenableBuilder(
              listenable: controller,
              builder: (context, child) {
                return Row(
                  mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                  children: [
                    if (controller.hasDateFilter)
                      IconButton(
                        key: const ValueKey<String>(
                          'listening-history-clear-filter',
                        ),
                        tooltip: 'Clear date filter',
                        onPressed: controller.isLoading
                            ? null
                            : () {
                                unawaited(controller.clearDateRange());
                              },
                        icon: const Icon(Icons.filter_alt_off_rounded),
                      ),
                    IconButton(
                      key: const ValueKey<String>(
                        'listening-history-filter-button',
                      ),
                      tooltip: 'Filter by date',
                      onPressed: controller.isLoading
                          ? null
                          : () {
                              unawaited(_chooseDateRange(controller));
                            },
                      icon: Icon(
                        controller.hasDateFilter
                            ? Icons.filter_alt_rounded
                            : Icons.filter_alt_outlined,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                );
              },
            ),
        ],
      ),
      body: controller == null
          ? const _InitializingState()
          : ListenableBuilder(
              listenable: controller,
              builder: (context, child) {
                return _HistoryBody(controller: controller);
              },
            ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _chooseDateRange(ListeningHistoryController controller) async {
    final now = DateTime.now();

    final initialStart = controller.startedAtOrAfter?.toLocal();
    final initialBefore = controller.startedBefore?.toLocal();

    final initialRange = initialStart != null && initialBefore != null
        ? DateTimeRange(
            start: DateTime(
              initialStart.year,
              initialStart.month,
              initialStart.day,
            ),
            end: DateTime(
              initialBefore.year,
              initialBefore.month,
              initialBefore.day,
            ).subtract(const Duration(days: 1)),
          )
        : null;

    final range = await showDateRangePicker(
      context: context,
      firstDate: DateTime(1970),
      lastDate: DateTime(now.year + 1),
      initialDateRange: initialRange,
    );

    if (range == null || !mounted) {
      return;
    }

    final startLocal = DateTime(
      range.start.year,
      range.start.month,
      range.start.day,
    );

    final endExclusiveLocal = DateTime(
      range.end.year,
      range.end.month,
      range.end.day,
    ).add(const Duration(days: 1));

    await controller.setDateRange(
      startedAtOrAfter: startLocal.toUtc(),
      startedBefore: endExclusiveLocal.toUtc(),
    );
  }

  void _disposeOwnedController() {
    if (_ownsController) {
      _controller?.dispose();
    }

    _ownsController = false;
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _disposeOwnedController();
    super.dispose();
  }
}

class _InitializingState extends StatelessWidget {
  const _InitializingState();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return const Center(
      key: ValueKey<String>('listening-history-initializing'),
      child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
    );
  }
}

class _HistoryBody extends StatelessWidget {
  const _HistoryBody({required this.controller});

  final ListeningHistoryController controller;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (controller.isLoading && controller.entries.isEmpty) {
      return const Center(
        key: ValueKey<String>('listening-history-loading'),
        child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
      );
    }

    if (controller.hasError && controller.entries.isEmpty) {
      return _HistoryErrorState(controller: controller);
    }

    if (controller.isEmpty) {
      return RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: controller.refresh,
        child: ListView(
          key: const ValueKey<String>('listening-history-empty'),
          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          children: const [
            SizedBox(height: 180),
            Icon(Icons.history_rounded, size: 48),
            SizedBox(height: 16),
            Center(
              child: Text(
                'No listening history yet.',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 8),
            Center(
              child: Padding(
                padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 32),
                child: Text(
                  'Songs you listen to will appear here, including sessions that do not qualify as streams.',
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return RefreshIndicator(
      onRefresh: controller.refresh,
      child: ListView.builder(
        key: const ValueKey<String>('listening-history-list'),
        physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 12, 12, 24),
        itemCount:
            controller.entries.length +
            (controller.hasMore || controller.isLoadingMore ? 1 : 0),
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          if (index < controller.entries.length) {
            return _HistoryEntryCard(entry: controller.entries[index]);
          }

          return _LoadMoreSection(controller: controller);
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _HistoryErrorState extends StatelessWidget {
  const _HistoryErrorState({required this.controller});

  final ListeningHistoryController controller;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      key: const ValueKey<String>('listening-history-error'),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.error_outline_rounded, size: 48),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
              'Could not load listening history.',
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            const Text('Try again in a moment.', textAlign: TextAlign.center),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            FilledButton.icon(
              key: const ValueKey<String>('listening-history-retry'),
              onPressed: controller.isLoading
                  ? null
                  : () {
                      unawaited(controller.refresh());
                    },
              icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded),
              label: const Text('Retry'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _HistoryEntryCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _HistoryEntryCard({required this.entry});

  final ListeningHistoryEntry entry;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final session = entry.session;
    final theme = Theme.of(context);

    final localStartedAt = session.startedAt.toLocal();
    final localizations = MaterialLocalizations.of(context);

    final dateText = localizations.formatMediumDate(localStartedAt);
    final timeText = localizations.formatTimeOfDay(
      TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(localStartedAt),
    );

    return Card(
      key: ValueKey<String>('listening-history-entry-${session.id}'),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        session.songTitle,
                        key: ValueKey<String>(
                          'listening-history-title-${session.id}',
                        ),
                        style: theme.textTheme.titleMedium?.copyWith(
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 3),
                      Text(
                        session.artistName,
                        key: ValueKey<String>(
                          'listening-history-artist-${session.id}',
                        ),
                        style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium?.copyWith(
                          color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                _QualificationBadge(entry: entry),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            Text(
              _albumLabel(session),
              key: ValueKey<String>('listening-history-album-${session.id}'),
              style: theme.textTheme.bodyMedium,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text(
              '$dateText at $timeText',
              key: ValueKey<String>('listening-history-time-${session.id}'),
              style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Wrap(
              spacing: 12,
              runSpacing: 4,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Listened ${_formatDuration(session.listenedDuration)}',
                  key: ValueKey<String>(
                    'listening-history-duration-${session.id}',
                  ),
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall,
                ),
                Text(
                  _endReasonLabel(session.endReason),
                  key: ValueKey<String>(
                    'listening-history-end-reason-${session.id}',
                  ),
                  style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall,
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (entry.qualifiedAt != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(
                _qualifiedAtLabel(context, entry.qualifiedAt!),
                key: ValueKey<String>(
                  'listening-history-qualified-at-${session.id}',
                ),
                style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                ),
              ),
            ],
            if (session.sourceContext?.trim().isNotEmpty == true) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(
                'Source: ${session.sourceContext}',
                key: ValueKey<String>('listening-history-source-${session.id}'),
                style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String _albumLabel(PlaybackSession session) {
    final edition = session.albumEditionTitle.trim();

    if (edition.isEmpty || edition == session.albumTitle.trim()) {
      return session.albumTitle;
    }

    return '${session.albumTitle} â€¢ $edition';
  }
}

class _QualificationBadge extends StatelessWidget {
  const _QualificationBadge({required this.entry});

  final ListeningHistoryEntry entry;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final qualified = entry.isQualified;

    return Chip(
      key: ValueKey<String>('listening-history-qualified-${entry.session.id}'),
      avatar: Icon(
        qualified ? Icons.check_circle_rounded : Icons.remove_circle_outline,
        size: 18,
      ),
      label: Text(qualified ? 'Qualified' : 'Not qualified'),
      visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact,
    );
  }
}

class _LoadMoreSection extends StatelessWidget {
  const _LoadMoreSection({required this.controller});

  final ListeningHistoryController controller;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (controller.isLoadingMore) {
      return const Padding(
        key: ValueKey<String>('listening-history-loading-more'),
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      );
    }

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 12),
      child: Center(
        child: OutlinedButton.icon(
          key: const ValueKey<String>('listening-history-load-more'),
          onPressed: () {
            unawaited(controller.loadMore());
          },
          icon: const Icon(Icons.expand_more_rounded),
          label: const Text('Load more'),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

String _qualifiedAtLabel(BuildContext context, DateTime qualifiedAt) {
  final local = qualifiedAt.toLocal();
  final localizations = MaterialLocalizations.of(context);

  return 'Qualified ${localizations.formatMediumDate(local)} at '
      '${localizations.formatTimeOfDay(TimeOfDay.fromDateTime(local))}';
}

String _formatDuration(Duration duration) {
  final totalSeconds = duration.inSeconds.clamp(0, 1 << 31);

  final hours = totalSeconds ~/ 3600;
  final minutes = (totalSeconds % 3600) ~/ 60;
  final seconds = totalSeconds % 60;

  if (hours > 0) {
    return '${hours}h ${minutes}m ${seconds}s';
  }

  if (minutes > 0) {
    return '${minutes}m ${seconds}s';
  }

  return '${seconds}s';
}

String _endReasonLabel(PlaybackSessionEndReason? reason) {
  return switch (reason) {
    PlaybackSessionEndReason.completed => 'Completed',
    PlaybackSessionEndReason.skipped => 'Skipped',
    PlaybackSessionEndReason.stopped => 'Stopped',
    PlaybackSessionEndReason.replaced => 'Replaced',
    PlaybackSessionEndReason.interrupted => 'Interrupted',
    PlaybackSessionEndReason.failed => 'Failed',
    null => 'In progress',
  };
}

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../../../../core/database/app_database.dart';
import '../../../player/application/controllers/playback_coordinator.dart';
import '../../application/controllers/playback_analytics_controller.dart';
import '../../application/services/listening_session_recovery_service.dart';
import '../../application/services/listening_session_tracker.dart';
import '../../data/daos/playback_listening_segment_dao.dart';
import '../../data/daos/playback_session_dao.dart';
import '../../data/daos/qualified_play_dao.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/listening_session_mutation_transaction_runner.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/sqlite_playback_listening_segment_repository.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/sqlite_playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../../data/repositories/sqlite_qualified_play_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_listening_segment_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';

typedef AnalyticsScopeNow = DateTime Function();

class AnalyticsRuntime {
  const AnalyticsRuntime({
    required this.playbackSessions,
    required this.listeningSegments,
    required this.qualifiedPlays,
    required this.controller,
  });

  final PlaybackSessionRepository playbackSessions;
  final PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository listeningSegments;
  final QualifiedPlayRepository qualifiedPlays;
  final PlaybackAnalyticsController controller;

  static Future<AnalyticsRuntime> create({
    required AppDatabase appDatabase,
    required PlaybackCoordinator playbackCoordinator,
    AnalyticsScopeNow? now,
    PlaybackAnalyticsUserIdProvider? userIdProvider,
  }) async {
    final database = await appDatabase.database;

    final playbackSessions = SqlitePlaybackSessionRepository(
      PlaybackSessionDao(database),
    );

    final listeningSegments = SqlitePlaybackListeningSegmentRepository(
      PlaybackListeningSegmentDao(database),
    );

    final qualifiedPlays = SqliteQualifiedPlayRepository(
      QualifiedPlayDao(database),
    );

    final recovery = ListeningSessionRecoveryService(
      playbackSessions: playbackSessions,
      listeningSegments: listeningSegments,
    );

    await recovery.recoverInterruptedSessions();

    final tracker = ListeningSessionTracker(
      playbackSessions: playbackSessions,
      listeningSegments: listeningSegments,
      qualifiedPlays: qualifiedPlays,
      mutationRunner: ListeningSessionMutationTransactionRunner(
        appDatabase: appDatabase,
      ),
      now: now,
    );

    final controller = PlaybackAnalyticsController(
      playbackCoordinator: playbackCoordinator,
      tracker: tracker,
      now: now,
      userIdProvider: userIdProvider,
    );

    return AnalyticsRuntime(
      playbackSessions: playbackSessions,
      listeningSegments: listeningSegments,
      qualifiedPlays: qualifiedPlays,
      controller: controller,
    );
  }

  Future<void> dispose() => controller.dispose();
}

class AnalyticsScope extends StatefulWidget {
  const AnalyticsScope({
    super.key,
    required this.child,
    this.now,
    this.userIdProvider,
  });

  final Widget child;
  final AnalyticsScopeNow? now;
  final PlaybackAnalyticsUserIdProvider? userIdProvider;

  @override
  State<AnalyticsScope> createState() => _AnalyticsScopeState();
}

class _AnalyticsScopeState extends State<AnalyticsScope> {
  AppDatabase? _database;
  PlaybackCoordinator? _playbackCoordinator;
  AnalyticsRuntime? _runtime;

  int _initializationVersion = 0;

  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();

    final database = context.watch<AppDatabase>();
    final playbackCoordinator = context.read<PlaybackCoordinator>();

    if (identical(database, _database) &&
        identical(playbackCoordinator, _playbackCoordinator)) {
      return;
    }

    _database = database;
    _playbackCoordinator = playbackCoordinator;

    final previousRuntime = _runtime;
    _runtime = null;

    if (previousRuntime != null) {
      unawaited(previousRuntime.dispose());
    }

    final version = ++_initializationVersion;

    unawaited(
      _initializeAnalytics(
        appDatabase: database,
        playbackCoordinator: playbackCoordinator,
        version: version,
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _initializeAnalytics({
    required AppDatabase appDatabase,
    required PlaybackCoordinator playbackCoordinator,
    required int version,
  }) async {
    final runtime = await AnalyticsRuntime.create(
      appDatabase: appDatabase,
      playbackCoordinator: playbackCoordinator,
      now: widget.now,
      userIdProvider: widget.userIdProvider,
    );

    if (!mounted || version != _initializationVersion) {
      await runtime.dispose();
      return;
    }

    setState(() {
      _runtime = runtime;
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final runtime = _runtime;

    return MultiProvider(
      providers: [
        Provider<PlaybackSessionRepository?>.value(
          value: runtime?.playbackSessions,
        ),
        Provider<PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository?>.value(
          value: runtime?.listeningSegments,
        ),
        Provider<QualifiedPlayRepository?>.value(
          value: runtime?.qualifiedPlays,
        ),
        Provider<PlaybackAnalyticsController?>.value(
          value: runtime?.controller,
        ),
      ],
      child: widget.child,
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _initializationVersion++;

    final runtime = _runtime;

    if (runtime != null) {
      unawaited(runtime.dispose());
    }

    super.dispose();
  }
}

class HomeSnapshot {

    required this.recentlyAddedTracks,
    required this.recentlyAddedAlbums,
    required this.weeklyPopularTracks,
    required this.weeklyTopArtists,
  });

  final List<LibraryTrack> recentlyAddedTracks;
  final List<LibraryAlbum> recentlyAddedAlbums;

  final List<HomeTopArtist> weeklyTopArtists;

  bool get isEmpty =>
      recentlyAddedTracks.isEmpty &&
      recentlyAddedAlbums.isEmpty &&
      weeklyPopularTracks.isEmpty &&
      weeklyTopArtists.isEmpty;
}

===== lib\features\home\application\services\home_data_service.dart =====
import '../../../analytics/application/models/analytics_period.dart';
import '../../../analytics/application/models/analytics_ranked_entity.dart';
import '../../../analytics/application/services/analytics_artist_listening_duration_service.dart';
import '../../../analytics/application/services/analytics_entity_ranking_service.dart';
import '../../../library/application/models/library_album.dart';
import '../../../library/application/models/library_track.dart';
import '../../../library/application/models/library_track_order.dart';
import '../../../library/application/repositories/library_query_repository.dart';
import '../models/home_popular_track.dart';
import '../models/home_snapshot.dart';
import '../models/home_top_artist.dart';

class HomeDataService {
  const HomeDataService({
    required this._library,
    required this._analyticsRankings,
    required this._artistListeningDurations,
    this.sectionLimit = 6,
  });

  final LibraryQueryRepository _library;
  final AnalyticsEntityRankingService _analyticsRankings;
  final AnalyticsArtistListeningDurationService _artistListeningDurations;

  final int sectionLimit;

  Future<HomeSnapshot> load({required DateTime referenceLocal}) async {
    if (sectionLimit <= 0) {
      throw StateError('Home section limit must be greater than zero.');
    }

    final recentlyAddedTracksFuture = _library.getTracks(
      order: LibraryTrackOrder.dateAdded,
    );

    final albumsFuture = _library.getAlbums();

    final rankingsFuture = _analyticsRankings.load(
      period: AnalyticsPeriod.week,
      referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
    );

    final artistDurationsFuture = _artistListeningDurations.load(
      period: AnalyticsPeriod.week,
      referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
    );

    final recentlyAddedTracks = await recentlyAddedTracksFuture;
    final albums = await albumsFuture;
    final rankings = await rankingsFuture;
    final artistDurations = await artistDurationsFuture;

    final availableTracks = recentlyAddedTracks
        .where(_hasAvailableMedia)
        .toList(growable: false);

    final visibleRecentlyAddedTracks = availableTracks
        .take(sectionLimit)
        .toList(growable: false);

    final availableAlbumIds = availableTracks
        .map((track) => track.albumId)
        .toSet();

    final availableArtistIds = availableTracks
        .map((track) => track.artistId)
        .toSet();

    for (final album in albums) {
      if (availableAlbumIds.contains(album.albumId)) {
        availableArtistIds.add(album.artistId);
      }
    }

    final visibleRecentlyAddedAlbums =
        albums
            .where((album) => availableAlbumIds.contains(album.albumId))
            .toList(growable: false)
          ..sort(_compareRecentlyAddedAlbums);

    final popularTracks = await _buildPopularTracks(rankings.songs);

    final topArtists = await _buildTopArtists(
      rankings.artists,
      artistDurations,
      availableArtistIds,
    );

    return HomeSnapshot(
      recentlyAddedTracks: List.unmodifiable(visibleRecentlyAddedTracks),
      recentlyAddedAlbums: List.unmodifiable(
        visibleRecentlyAddedAlbums.take(sectionLimit),
      ),
      weeklyPopularTracks: List.unmodifiable(popularTracks),
      weeklyTopArtists: List.unmodifiable(topArtists),
    );
  }

  Future<List<HomePopularTrack>> _buildPopularTracks(
    List<AnalyticsRankedEntity> rankings,
  ) async {
    final result = <HomePopularTrack>[];

    for (final ranking in rankings) {
      if (result.length >= sectionLimit) {
        break;
      }

      final track = await _library.getTrackById(ranking.entityId);

      if (track == null || track.isBlocked) {
        continue;
      }

      result.add(
        HomePopularTrack(
          track: track,
          rank: result.length + 1,
          qualifiedStreamCount: ranking.qualifiedStreamCount,
        ),
      );
    }

    return result;
  }

  Future<List<HomeTopArtist>> _buildTopArtists(
    List<AnalyticsRankedEntity> rankings,
    Map<String, Duration> artistDurations,
    Set<String> availableArtistIds,
  ) async {
    final result = <HomeTopArtist>[];

    for (final ranking in rankings) {
      if (result.length >= sectionLimit) {
        break;
      }

      final artist = await _library.getArtistById(ranking.entityId);

      if (artist == null) {
        continue;
      }

      result.add(
        HomeTopArtist(
          artist: artist,
          rank: result.length + 1,
          qualifiedStreamCount: ranking.qualifiedStreamCount,
          weeklyListeningDuration:
              artistDurations[ranking.entityId] ?? Duration.zero,
          isAvailable: availableArtistIds.contains(artist.artistId),
        ),
      );
    }

    return result;
  }

  static bool _hasAvailableMedia(LibraryTrack track) {
    return track.isAvailable &&
        track.filePath != null &&
        track.filePath!.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  static int _compareRecentlyAddedAlbums(LibraryAlbum a, LibraryAlbum b) {
    final createdCompare = b.dateAdded.compareTo(a.dateAdded);

    if (createdCompare != 0) {
      return createdCompare;
    }

    final titleCompare = a.title.toLowerCase().compareTo(b.title.toLowerCase());

    if (titleCompare != 0) {
      return titleCompare;
    }

    return a.albumId.compareTo(b.albumId);
  }
}

===== lib\features\home\presentation\controllers\home_controller.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../application/models/home_snapshot.dart';
import '../../application/services/home_data_service.dart';

typedef HomeControllerNow = DateTime Function();

class HomeController extends ChangeNotifier {
  HomeController({required this._service, HomeControllerNow? now})
    : _now = now ?? DateTime.now;

  final HomeDataService _service;
  final HomeControllerNow _now;

  HomeSnapshot? _snapshot;
  bool _isLoading = false;
  Object? _error;

  int _requestGeneration = 0;

  HomeSnapshot? get snapshot => _snapshot;

  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;

  Object? get error => _error;

  bool get hasError => _error != null;

  bool get hasData => _snapshot != null;

  bool get isEmpty =>
      !_isLoading &&
      _error == null &&
      (_snapshot == null || _snapshot!.isEmpty);

  Future<void> load() {
    if (_isLoading) {
      return Future<void>.value();
    }

    return _load();
  }

  Future<void> refresh() {
    return _load();
  }

  Future<void> _load() async {
    final generation = ++_requestGeneration;

    _isLoading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final snapshot = await _service.load(referenceLocal: _now());

      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _snapshot = snapshot;
      _error = null;
    } catch (error) {
      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _error = error;
    } finally {
      if (generation == _requestGeneration) {
        _isLoading = false;
        notifyListeners();
      }
    }
  }
}

import '../../../library/presentation/pages/artist_profile_page.dart';
import '../../../player/application/controllers/playback_coordinator.dart';
import '../../../playlists/presentation/widgets/add_to_playlist_sheet.dart';
import '../../application/models/home_popular_track.dart';
import '../../application/models/home_top_artist.dart';
import '../controllers/home_controller.dart';

class HomePage extends StatelessWidget {
  const HomePage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final controller = context.watch<HomeController?>();

    if (controller == null) {
      return const _HomeMessage(
        icon: Icons.hourglass_empty_rounded,
        title: 'Home is getting ready',
        message: 'Your Sonus Home data is still initializing.',
      );
    }

    final snapshot = controller.snapshot;

    if (controller.isLoading && snapshot == null) {
      return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
    }

    if (controller.hasError && snapshot == null) {
      return _HomeError(onRetry: controller.load);
    }

    if (snapshot == null || snapshot.isEmpty) {
      return RefreshIndicator(
        onRefresh: controller.refresh,
        child: ListView(
          physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 28, 20, 40),
          children: const [
            SizedBox(height: 100),
            _HomeMessage(
              icon: Icons.home_outlined,
              title: 'Your Home is empty',
              message: 'Import music to get started.',
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    return RefreshIndicator(
      onRefresh: controller.refresh,
      child: ListView(
        key: const PageStorageKey<String>('home-scroll'),
        physics: const AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics(),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(0, 20, 0, 40),
        children: [
          if (controller.hasError)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 0, 20, 12),
              child: _RefreshErrorBanner(onRetry: controller.refresh),
            ),

          if (snapshot.weeklyPopularTracks.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const _SectionHeading(title: 'Popular This Week'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _PopularTracksRail(entries: snapshot.weeklyPopularTracks),
            const SizedBox(height: 30),
          ],

          if (snapshot.weeklyTopArtists.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const _SectionHeading(title: 'Top Artists'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _TopArtistsRail(entries: snapshot.weeklyTopArtists),
            const SizedBox(height: 30),
          ],

          if (snapshot.recentlyAddedTracks.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const _SectionHeading(title: 'Recently Added'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _RecentlyAddedTracksRail(tracks: snapshot.recentlyAddedTracks),
            const SizedBox(height: 30),
          ],

          if (snapshot.recentlyAddedAlbums.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const _SectionHeading(title: 'New Albums'),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            _AlbumsRail(albums: snapshot.recentlyAddedAlbums),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SectionHeading extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SectionHeading({required this.title});

  final String title;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
      child: Text(
        title,
        style: Theme.of(
          context,
        ).textTheme.titleLarge?.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _PopularTracksRail extends StatelessWidget {
  const _PopularTracksRail({required this.entries});

  final List<HomePopularTrack> entries;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SizedBox(
      height: 270,
      child: ListView.separated(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 20),
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        itemCount: entries.length,
        separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const SizedBox(width: 14),
        itemBuilder: (context, index) {
          final entry = entries[index];

          return _PopularTrackCard(
            entry: entry,
            onTap: () {
              unawaited(_showTrackContext(context, entry.track));
            },
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _PopularTrackCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _PopularTrackCard({required this.entry, required this.onTap});

  final HomePopularTrack entry;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final track = entry.track;

    return SizedBox(
      width: 170,
      child: InkWell(
        key: ValueKey<String>('home-popular-track-${track.songId}'),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 6),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Stack(
                children: [
                  _SquareArtwork(
                    path: track.artworkPath,
                    fallbackIcon: Icons.music_note_rounded,
                    size: 170,
                  ),
                  Positioned(
                    top: 10,
                    left: 10,
                    child: _RankBadge(rank: entry.rank),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Text(
                track.title,
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: theme.textTheme.titleSmall?.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 3),
              Text(
                track.artistName,
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 3),
              Text(
                '${entry.qualifiedStreamCount} weekly streams',
                style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall?.copyWith(
                  color: theme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,

            },

      ),
    );

}

        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,

        separatorBuilder: (_, _) => const SizedBox(width: 14),

}

class _RecentTrackCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _RecentTrackCard({required this.track, required this.onTap});

  final LibraryTrack track;
  final VoidCallback? onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final theme = Theme.of(context);
    final isDisabled = track.isBlocked;

    return SizedBox(
      width: 150,
      child: InkWell(
        key: ValueKey<String>('home-recent-track-${track.songId}'),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Opacity(
          opacity: isDisabled ? 0.55 : 1,
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Stack(
                children: [
                  _SquareArtwork(
                    path: track.artworkPath,
                    fallbackIcon: Icons.music_note_rounded,
                    size: 150,
                  ),
                  if (isDisabled)
                    Positioned.fill(
                      child: DecoratedBox(
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: theme.colorScheme.surface.withValues(
                            alpha: 0.45,
                          ),
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                        ),
                        child: const Center(child: Icon(Icons.block_rounded)),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 10),
              Text(
                track.title,
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: theme.textTheme.titleSmall?.copyWith(
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 3),
              Text(
                track.artistName,
                maxLines: 1,
                overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                style: theme.textTheme.bodySmall,
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SquareArtwork extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SquareArtwork({
    required this.path,
    required this.fallbackIcon,
    required this.size,
  });

  final String? path;
  final IconData fallbackIcon;
  final double size;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final normalizedPath = path?.trim();

    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
      child: SizedBox.square(
        dimension: size,
        child: normalizedPath != null && normalizedPath.isNotEmpty
            ? Image.file(
                File(normalizedPath),
                fit: BoxFit.cover,
                errorBuilder: (_, _, _) => _ArtworkFallback(icon: fallbackIcon),
              )
            : _ArtworkFallback(icon: fallbackIcon),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ArtworkFallback extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ArtworkFallback({required this.icon});

  final IconData icon;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colorScheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;

    return ColoredBox(
      color: colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest,
      child: Center(
        child: Icon(icon, size: 42, color: colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ArtistArtwork extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ArtistArtwork({required this.artist, required this.size});

  final LibraryArtist artist;
  final double size;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final topArtistsPath = artist.topArtistsArtworkPath?.trim();
    final headerPath = artist.headerArtworkPath?.trim();

    final path = topArtistsPath != null && topArtistsPath.isNotEmpty
        ? topArtistsPath
        : headerPath != null && headerPath.isNotEmpty
        ? headerPath
        : null;

    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(size / 2),
      child: SizedBox.square(
        dimension: size,
        child: path == null
            ? const _ArtworkFallback(icon: Icons.person_rounded)
            : Image.file(
                File(path),
                key: ValueKey<String>(
                  'home-top-artist-artwork-${artist.artistId}',
                ),
                fit: BoxFit.cover,
                errorBuilder: (_, _, _) =>
                    const _ArtworkFallback(icon: Icons.person_rounded),
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AlbumArtwork extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AlbumArtwork({required this.album, required this.size});

  final LibraryAlbum album;
  final double size;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final path = album.artworkPath?.trim();

    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
      child: SizedBox.square(
        dimension: size,
        child: path == null || path.isEmpty
            ? const _ArtworkFallback(icon: Icons.album_rounded)
            : Image.file(
                File(path),
                key: ValueKey<String>(
                  'home-new-album-artwork-${album.albumId}',
                ),
                fit: BoxFit.cover,
                errorBuilder: (_, _, _) =>
                    const _ArtworkFallback(icon: Icons.album_rounded),
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

}

class _HomeError extends StatelessWidget {
  const _HomeError({required this.onRetry});

  final Future<void> Function() onRetry;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Center(
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const Icon(Icons.error_outline_rounded, size: 42),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            const Text('Home could not be loaded.'),
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            FilledButton.tonal(
              onPressed: () {
                unawaited(onRetry());
              },
              child: const Text('Retry'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _RefreshErrorBanner extends StatelessWidget {
  const _RefreshErrorBanner({required this.onRetry});

  final Future<void> Function() onRetry;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colorScheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;

    return Material(
      color: colorScheme.errorContainer,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 10, 8, 10),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            Icon(
              Icons.error_outline_rounded,
              color: colorScheme.onErrorContainer,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 10),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                'Home could not refresh.',
                style: TextStyle(color: colorScheme.onErrorContainer),
              ),
            ),
            TextButton(
              onPressed: () {
                unawaited(onRetry());
              },
              child: const Text('Retry'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

Future<void> _showTrackContext(BuildContext context, LibraryTrack track) async {
  if (track.isBlocked || !track.isAvailable) {
    return;
  }

  final playback = context.read<PlaybackCoordinator?>();

  await showModalBottomSheet<void>(
    context: context,
    showDragHandle: true,
    builder: (sheetContext) {
      return SafeArea(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 8),
          child: ListView(
            shrinkWrap: true,
            children: [
              ListTile(
                title: Text(
                  track.title,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
                subtitle: Text(
                  '${track.artistName} ï¿½ ${track.albumTitle}',
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                ),
              ),
              const Divider(height: 1),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.play_arrow_rounded),
                title: const Text('Play'),
                enabled: playback != null,
                onTap: playback == null
                    ? null
                    : () {
                        Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();
                        unawaited(playback.playTrack(track));
                      },
              ),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.playlist_play_rounded),
                title: const Text('Play next'),
                enabled: playback != null,
                onTap: playback == null
                    ? null
                    : () {
                        Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();
                        unawaited(playback.playNext(track));
                      },
              ),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.queue_music_rounded),
                title: const Text('Add to queue'),
                enabled: playback != null,
                onTap: playback == null
                    ? null
                    : () {
                        Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();
                        unawaited(playback.addToQueue(track));
                      },
              ),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.playlist_add_rounded),
                title: const Text('Add to playlist'),
                onTap: () {
                  Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();
                  unawaited(showAddToPlaylistFlow(context, track));
                },
              ),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.album_outlined),
                title: const Text('Go to album'),
                onTap: () {
                  Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();

                  Navigator.of(context).push(
                    MaterialPageRoute<void>(
                      builder: (_) => AlbumProfilePage(
                        albumId: track.albumId,
                        initialAlbumTitle: track.albumTitle,
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
              ListTile(
                leading: const Icon(Icons.person_outline_rounded),
                title: const Text('Go to artist'),
                onTap: () {
                  Navigator.of(sheetContext).pop();

                  Navigator.of(context).push(
                    MaterialPageRoute<void>(
                      builder: (_) => ArtistProfilePage(
                        artistId: track.artistId,
                        initialArtistName: track.artistName,
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
  );
}

String _formatListeningDuration(Duration duration) {
  final totalMinutes = duration.inMinutes;

  if (totalMinutes < 60) {
    return '$totalMinutes min listening';
  }

  final hours = totalMinutes ~/ 60;
  final minutes = totalMinutes % 60;

  if (minutes == 0) {
    return '${hours}h listening';
  }

  return '${hours}h ${minutes}m listening';
}

===== lib\features\home\presentation\scopes\home_scope.dart =====
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/widgets.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../../../analytics/application/services/analytics_artist_listening_duration_service.dart';
import '../../../analytics/application/services/analytics_entity_ranking_service.dart';
import '../../../analytics/domain/repositories/playback_listening_segment_repository.dart';
import '../../../analytics/domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../../../analytics/domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';
import '../../../library/application/repositories/library_query_repository.dart';
import '../../application/services/home_data_service.dart';
import '../controllers/home_controller.dart';

class HomeScope extends StatefulWidget {
  const HomeScope({super.key, required this.child, this.now});

  final Widget child;
  final HomeControllerNow? now;

  @override
  State<HomeScope> createState() => _HomeScopeState();
}

class _HomeScopeState extends State<HomeScope> {
  LibraryQueryRepository? _library;
  QualifiedPlayRepository? _qualifiedPlays;
  PlaybackSessionRepository? _playbackSessions;
  PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository? _listeningSegments;

  HomeController? _controller;

  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();

    final library = context.watch<LibraryQueryRepository?>();
    final qualifiedPlays = context.watch<QualifiedPlayRepository?>();
    final playbackSessions = context.watch<PlaybackSessionRepository?>();
    final listeningSegments = context
        .watch<PlaybackListeningSegmentRepository?>();

    if (identical(library, _library) &&
        identical(qualifiedPlays, _qualifiedPlays) &&
        identical(playbackSessions, _playbackSessions) &&
        identical(listeningSegments, _listeningSegments)) {
      return;
    }

    _library = library;
    _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays;
    _playbackSessions = playbackSessions;
    _listeningSegments = listeningSegments;

    _recreateController();
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(HomeScope oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);

    if (!identical(oldWidget.now, widget.now)) {
      _recreateController();
    }
  }

  void _recreateController() {
    final previousController = _controller;
    _controller = null;

    previousController?.dispose();

    final library = _library;
    final qualifiedPlays = _qualifiedPlays;
    final playbackSessions = _playbackSessions;
    final listeningSegments = _listeningSegments;

    if (library == null ||
        qualifiedPlays == null ||
        playbackSessions == null ||
        listeningSegments == null) {
      return;
    }

    final service = HomeDataService(
      library: library,
      analyticsRankings: AnalyticsEntityRankingService(
        qualifiedPlays: qualifiedPlays,
      ),
      artistListeningDurations: AnalyticsArtistListeningDurationService(
        playbackSessions: playbackSessions,
        listeningSegments: listeningSegments,
      ),
    );

    final controller = HomeController(service: service, now: widget.now);

    _controller = controller;

    unawaited(controller.load());
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ChangeNotifierProvider<HomeController?>.value(
      value: _controller,
      child: widget.child,
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _controller?.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }
}
