===== lib\features\charts\application\services\chart_finalization_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../../analytics/domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/chart_entry.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/chart_snapshot.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_type.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_week.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_artist_achievement_detector.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_finalized_detector.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_lifetime_stream_service.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_longevity_calculator.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_milestone_detector.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/chart_moment_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/chart_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/services/chart_week_resolver.dart';
import 'chart_snapshot_builder_service.dart';

typedef ChartFinalizationNow = DateTime Function();

class ChartFinalizationService {
  ChartFinalizationService({
    required ChartSnapshotBuilderService builder,
    required ChartRepository charts,
    required QualifiedPlayRepository qualifiedPlays,
    ChartMomentRepository? moments,
    ChartMomentFinalizedDetector finalizedMomentDetector =
        const ChartMomentFinalizedDetector(),
    ChartMomentArtistAchievementDetector artistAchievementDetector =
        const ChartMomentArtistAchievementDetector(),
    ChartMomentLongevityCalculator longevityCalculator =
        const ChartMomentLongevityCalculator(),
    ChartMomentMilestoneDetector milestoneDetector =
        const ChartMomentMilestoneDetector(),
    ChartMomentLifetimeStreamService? lifetimeStreamService,
    DateTime? momentsActivationWeekStartUtc,
    ChartWeekResolver weekResolver = const ChartWeekResolver(),
    ChartFinalizationNow? now,
  }) : _builder = builder,
       _charts = charts,
       _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays,
       _moments = moments,
       _finalizedMomentDetector = finalizedMomentDetector,
       _artistAchievementDetector = artistAchievementDetector,
       _longevityCalculator = longevityCalculator,
       _milestoneDetector = milestoneDetector,
       _lifetimeStreamService =
           lifetimeStreamService ??
           ChartMomentLifetimeStreamService(qualifiedPlays: qualifiedPlays),
       _momentsActivationWeekStartUtc = momentsActivationWeekStartUtc?.toUtc(),
       _weekResolver = weekResolver,
       _now = now ?? DateTime.now;

  final ChartSnapshotBuilderService _builder;
  final ChartRepository _charts;
  final QualifiedPlayRepository _qualifiedPlays;

  final ChartMomentRepository? _moments;
  final ChartMomentFinalizedDetector _finalizedMomentDetector;
  final ChartMomentArtistAchievementDetector _artistAchievementDetector;
  final ChartMomentLongevityCalculator _longevityCalculator;
  final ChartMomentMilestoneDetector _milestoneDetector;
  final ChartMomentLifetimeStreamService _lifetimeStreamService;
  final DateTime? _momentsActivationWeekStartUtc;

  final ChartWeekResolver _weekResolver;
  final ChartFinalizationNow _now;

  Future<List<ChartSnapshot>> finalizeCompletedWeeks({
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
  }) async {
    final currentWeek = _weekResolver.resolve(referenceLocal);

    final plays = await _qualifiedPlays.getAll(
      qualifiedBefore: currentWeek.startUtc,
    );

    final weeksByStartUtc = <String, ChartWeek>{};

    for (final play in plays) {
      final week = _weekResolver.resolve(play.qualifiedAt.toLocal());

      if (!week.startUtc.isBefore(currentWeek.startUtc)) {
        continue;
      }

      weeksByStartUtc[week.startUtc.toIso8601String()] = week;
    }

    final weeks = weeksByStartUtc.values.toList()
      ..sort((a, b) => a.startUtc.compareTo(b.startUtc));

    final results = <ChartSnapshot>[];

    for (final week in weeks) {
      results.addAll(await reconcileCompletedWeek(week));
    }

    return List<ChartSnapshot>.unmodifiable(results);
  }

  Future<List<ChartSnapshot>> finalizePreviousCompletedWeek({
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
  }) {
    final previousWeekReference = DateTime(
      referenceLocal.year,
      referenceLocal.month,
      referenceLocal.day - 7,
      12,
    );

    final week = _weekResolver.resolve(previousWeekReference);

    return reconcileCompletedWeek(week);
  }

  Future<List<ChartSnapshot>> reconcileCompletedWeek(ChartWeek week) async {
    final results = <ChartSnapshot>[];

    for (final chartType in ChartType.values) {
      results.add(await _reconcileType(chartType: chartType, week: week));
    }

    return List<ChartSnapshot>.unmodifiable(results);
  }

  Future<ChartSnapshot> _reconcileType({
    required ChartType chartType,
    required ChartWeek week,
  }) async {
    final existing = await _charts.getForWeek(
      chartType: chartType,
      weekStartUtc: week.startUtc,
    );

    final candidate = await _builder.build(
      chartType: chartType,
      weekStartUtc: week.startUtc,
      weekEndUtc: week.endUtc,
      finalizedAt: week.endUtc,
      revision: existing == null ? 1 : existing.revision + 1,
    );

    late final ChartSnapshot canonical;

    if (existing != null && _sameChartContent(existing, candidate)) {
      canonical = existing;
    } else {
      await _charts.replaceSnapshot(candidate);
      canonical = candidate;
    }

    await _reconcileFinalizedMoments(canonical);

    return canonical;
  }

  Future<void> _reconcileFinalizedMoments(ChartSnapshot snapshot) async {
    final moments = _moments;
    final activationWeekStartUtc = _momentsActivationWeekStartUtc;

    if (moments == null || activationWeekStartUtc == null) {
      return;
    }

    if (snapshot.weekStartUtc.toUtc().isBefore(activationWeekStartUtc)) {
      return;
    }

    final history = await _charts.getHistory(snapshot.chartType);
    final createdAt = _now().toUtc();

    final events = _finalizedMomentDetector
        .detect(snapshot: snapshot, history: history, createdAt: createdAt)
        .toList();

    events.addAll(
      _artistAchievementDetector.detect(
        snapshot: snapshot,
        history: history,
        createdAt: createdAt,
      ),
    );

    final previousSnapshot =
        _immediatePreviousSnapshot(snapshot: snapshot, history: history) ??
        _emptyPreviousWeek(snapshot);

    final previousLifetimeStreams = await _lifetimeStreamService.loadBefore(
      chartType: snapshot.chartType,
      qualifiedBeforeUtc: snapshot.weekStartUtc,
    );

    final currentLifetimeStreams = await _lifetimeStreamService.load(
      snapshot: snapshot,
    );

    for (final entry in snapshot.entries) {
      final entityId = entry.entity.entityId;

      final previousLongevity = _longevityCalculator.calculate(
        entityId: entityId,
        snapshot: previousSnapshot,
        history: history,
      );

      final currentLongevity = _longevityCalculator.calculate(
        entityId: entityId,
        snapshot: snapshot,
        history: history,
      );

      events.addAll(
        _milestoneDetector.detect(
          snapshot: snapshot,
          entry: entry,
          previousLongevity: previousLongevity,
          currentLongevity: currentLongevity,
          previousLifetimeStreams: previousLifetimeStreams[entityId] ?? 0,
          currentLifetimeStreams: currentLifetimeStreams[entityId] ?? 0,
          createdAt: createdAt,
        ),
      );
    }

    await moments.replaceFinalizedForWeek(
      chartType: snapshot.chartType,
      weekStartUtc: snapshot.weekStartUtc,
      events: events,
    );
  }

  static ChartSnapshot? _immediatePreviousSnapshot({
    required ChartSnapshot snapshot,
    required Iterable<ChartSnapshot> history,
  }) {
    final matching = history
        .where(
          (historical) =>
              historical.isFinalized &&
              historical.chartType == snapshot.chartType &&
              historical.weekEndUtc.toUtc() == snapshot.weekStartUtc.toUtc(),
        )
        .toList(growable: false);

    if (matching.length > 1) {
      throw StateError(
        'Multiple finalized snapshots end at '
        '${snapshot.weekStartUtc.toUtc().toIso8601String()} '
        'for ${snapshot.chartType.name}.',
      );
    }

    return matching.isEmpty ? null : matching.single;
  }

  static ChartSnapshot _emptyPreviousWeek(ChartSnapshot snapshot) {
    final weekEndUtc = snapshot.weekStartUtc.toUtc();
    final weekStartUtc = weekEndUtc.subtract(const Duration(days: 7));

    return ChartSnapshot(
      id:
          'moment-baseline:${snapshot.chartType.name}:'
          '${weekStartUtc.millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      chartType: snapshot.chartType,
      weekStartUtc: weekStartUtc,
      weekEndUtc: weekEndUtc,
      finalizedAt: weekEndUtc,
      entries: const [],
    );
  }

  static bool _sameChartContent(
    ChartSnapshot existing,
    ChartSnapshot candidate,
  ) {
    if (existing.id != candidate.id ||
        existing.chartType != candidate.chartType ||
        existing.weekStartUtc.toUtc() != candidate.weekStartUtc.toUtc() ||
        existing.weekEndUtc.toUtc() != candidate.weekEndUtc.toUtc() ||
        existing.entries.length != candidate.entries.length) {
      return false;
    }

    for (var index = 0; index < existing.entries.length; index++) {
      if (!_sameEntry(existing.entries[index], candidate.entries[index])) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    return true;
  }

  static bool _sameEntry(ChartEntry existing, ChartEntry candidate) {
    return existing.entity.entityId == candidate.entity.entityId &&
        existing.entity.title == candidate.entity.title &&
        existing.entity.artistId == candidate.entity.artistId &&
        existing.entity.artistName == candidate.entity.artistName &&
        existing.entity.albumTitle == candidate.entity.albumTitle &&
        existing.rank == candidate.rank &&
        existing.qualifiedStreamCount == candidate.qualifiedStreamCount &&
        existing.lastQualifiedAt.toUtc() == candidate.lastQualifiedAt.toUtc() &&
        existing.previousFinalRank == candidate.previousFinalRank &&
        existing.peakRank == candidate.peakRank &&
        existing.peakAchievedAt.toUtc() == candidate.peakAchievedAt.toUtc() &&
        existing.weeksOnChart == candidate.weeksOnChart &&
        existing.movement == candidate.movement;
  }
}

===== lib\features\charts\application\services\chart_snapshot_builder_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../../analytics/domain/entities/playback_session.dart';
import '../../../analytics/domain/entities/qualified_play.dart';
import '../../../analytics/domain/repositories/playback_session_repository.dart';
import '../../../analytics/domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';
import '../../../library/application/models/library_album.dart';
import '../../../library/application/models/library_track.dart';
import '../../../library/application/repositories/library_query_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/chart_entry.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/chart_snapshot.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_entity_snapshot.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_ranked_entity.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_ranking_history.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_type.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/chart_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/services/chart_ranking_engine.dart';

class ChartSnapshotBuilderService {
  const ChartSnapshotBuilderService({
    required QualifiedPlayRepository qualifiedPlays,
    required LibraryQueryRepository library,
    required ChartRepository charts,
    PlaybackSessionRepository? playbackSessions,
    ChartRankingEngine rankingEngine = const ChartRankingEngine(),
  }) : _qualifiedPlays = qualifiedPlays,
       _library = library,
       _charts = charts,
       _playbackSessions = playbackSessions,
       _rankingEngine = rankingEngine;

  final QualifiedPlayRepository _qualifiedPlays;
  final LibraryQueryRepository _library;
  final ChartRepository _charts;
  final PlaybackSessionRepository? _playbackSessions;
  final ChartRankingEngine _rankingEngine;

  Future<ChartSnapshot> build({
    required ChartType chartType,
    required DateTime weekStartUtc,
    required DateTime weekEndUtc,
    DateTime? finalizedAt,
    int revision = 1,
    ChartRankObservationCallback? onRankObservation,
  }) async {
    final startUtc = weekStartUtc.toUtc();
    final endUtc = weekEndUtc.toUtc();

    if (!endUtc.isAfter(startUtc)) {
      throw ArgumentError('Chart week end must be after chart week start.');
    }

    if (revision < 1) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        revision,
        'revision',
        'Chart revision must be at least 1.',
      );
    }

    final plays = await _qualifiedPlays.getAll(
      qualifiedAtOrAfter: startUtc,
      qualifiedBefore: endUtc,
    );

    final history = await _charts.getHistory(chartType);

    final historyByEntityId = _buildHistoryContext(
      history: history,
      currentWeekStartUtc: startUtc,
    );

    final isHistoricalBuild = finalizedAt != null;

    Map<String, PlaybackSession> sessionsById = const {};

    if (isHistoricalBuild && plays.isNotEmpty) {
      final playbackSessions = _playbackSessions;

      if (playbackSessions == null) {
        throw StateError(
          'PlaybackSessionRepository is required for finalized chart builds.',
        );
      }

      final sessions = await playbackSessions.getByIds(
        plays.map((play) => play.playbackSessionId),
      );

      sessionsById = {for (final session in sessions) session.id: session};

      final missingSessionIds = plays
          .map((play) => play.playbackSessionId)
          .where((id) => !sessionsById.containsKey(id))
          .toSet();

      if (missingSessionIds.isNotEmpty) {
        throw StateError(
          'Cannot finalize chart with missing playback sessions: '
          '${missingSessionIds.join(', ')}',
        );
      }
    }

    switch (chartType) {
      case ChartType.songs:
        if (isHistoricalBuild) {
          return _buildHistoricalSongSnapshot(
            plays: plays,
            sessionsById: sessionsById,
            historyByEntityId: historyByEntityId,
            weekStartUtc: startUtc,
            weekEndUtc: endUtc,
            finalizedAt: finalizedAt,
            revision: revision,
          );
        }

        return _buildLiveSongSnapshot(
          plays: plays,
          historyByEntityId: historyByEntityId,
          weekStartUtc: startUtc,
          weekEndUtc: endUtc,
          revision: revision,
          onRankObservation: onRankObservation,
        );

      case ChartType.albums:
        if (isHistoricalBuild) {
          return _buildHistoricalAlbumSnapshot(
            plays: plays,
            sessionsById: sessionsById,
            historyByEntityId: historyByEntityId,
            weekStartUtc: startUtc,
            weekEndUtc: endUtc,
            finalizedAt: finalizedAt,
            revision: revision,
          );
        }

        return _buildLiveAlbumSnapshot(
          plays: plays,
          historyByEntityId: historyByEntityId,
          weekStartUtc: startUtc,
          weekEndUtc: endUtc,
          revision: revision,
          onRankObservation: onRankObservation,
        );
    }
  }

  Future<ChartSnapshot> _buildLiveSongSnapshot({
    required List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
    required Map<String, ChartRankingHistory> historyByEntityId,
    required DateTime weekStartUtc,
    required DateTime weekEndUtc,
    required int revision,
    ChartRankObservationCallback? onRankObservation,
  }) async {
    final tracks = await _library.getTracks();

    final trackById = <String, LibraryTrack>{
      for (final track in tracks) track.songId: track,
    };

    final eligibleSongIds = tracks
        .where((track) => !track.isBlocked)
        .map((track) => track.songId)
        .toSet();

    final ranked = _rankingEngine.rank(
      events: _eventsFor(plays: plays, chartType: ChartType.songs),
      historyByEntityId: historyByEntityId,
      eligibleEntityIds: eligibleSongIds,
      onRankObservation: onRankObservation,
    );

    final entries = <ChartEntry>[];

    for (final result in ranked) {
      final track = trackById[result.entityId];

      if (track == null || track.isBlocked) {
        continue;
      }

      entries.add(
        _entryFromRanked(
          result: result,
          entity: ChartEntitySnapshot(
            entityId: track.songId,
            title: track.title,
            artistId: track.artistId,
            artistName: track.artistName,
            albumTitle: track.albumTitle,
          ),
          isFinalized: false,
        ),
      );
    }

    return _snapshot(
      chartType: ChartType.songs,
      weekStartUtc: weekStartUtc,
      weekEndUtc: weekEndUtc,
      finalizedAt: null,
      revision: revision,
      entries: entries,
    );
  }

  Future<ChartSnapshot> _buildLiveAlbumSnapshot({
    required List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
    required Map<String, ChartRankingHistory> historyByEntityId,
    required DateTime weekStartUtc,
    required DateTime weekEndUtc,
    required int revision,
    ChartRankObservationCallback? onRankObservation,
  }) async {
    final albums = await _library.getAlbums();

    final albumById = <String, LibraryAlbum>{
      for (final album in albums) album.albumId: album,
    };

    final ranked = _rankingEngine.rank(
      events: _eventsFor(plays: plays, chartType: ChartType.albums),
      historyByEntityId: historyByEntityId,
      eligibleEntityIds: albumById.keys.toSet(),
      onRankObservation: onRankObservation,
    );

    final entries = <ChartEntry>[];

    for (final result in ranked) {
      final album = albumById[result.entityId];

      if (album == null) {
        continue;
      }

      entries.add(
        _entryFromRanked(
          result: result,
          entity: ChartEntitySnapshot(
            entityId: album.albumId,
            title: album.title,
            artistId: album.artistId,
            artistName: album.artistName,
          ),
          isFinalized: false,
        ),
      );
    }

    return _snapshot(
      chartType: ChartType.albums,
      weekStartUtc: weekStartUtc,
      weekEndUtc: weekEndUtc,
      finalizedAt: null,
      revision: revision,
      entries: entries,
    );
  }

  ChartSnapshot _buildHistoricalSongSnapshot({
    required List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
    required Map<String, PlaybackSession> sessionsById,
    required Map<String, ChartRankingHistory> historyByEntityId,
    required DateTime weekStartUtc,
    required DateTime weekEndUtc,
    required DateTime finalizedAt,
    required int revision,
  }) {
    final identityBySongId = _historicalIdentityByEntity(
      plays: plays,
      sessionsById: sessionsById,
      chartType: ChartType.songs,
    );

    final ranked = _rankingEngine.rank(
      events: _eventsFor(plays: plays, chartType: ChartType.songs),
      historyByEntityId: historyByEntityId,
      includeObservedPeakInResult: false,
    );

    final entries = ranked
        .map(
          (result) => _entryFromRanked(
            result: result,
            entity: identityBySongId[result.entityId]!,
            isFinalized: true,
          ),
        )
        .toList(growable: false);

    return _snapshot(
      chartType: ChartType.songs,
      weekStartUtc: weekStartUtc,
      weekEndUtc: weekEndUtc,
      finalizedAt: finalizedAt,
      revision: revision,
      entries: entries,
    );
  }

  ChartSnapshot _buildHistoricalAlbumSnapshot({
    required List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
    required Map<String, PlaybackSession> sessionsById,
    required Map<String, ChartRankingHistory> historyByEntityId,
    required DateTime weekStartUtc,
    required DateTime weekEndUtc,
    required DateTime finalizedAt,
    required int revision,
  }) {
    final identityByAlbumId = _historicalIdentityByEntity(
      plays: plays,
      sessionsById: sessionsById,
      chartType: ChartType.albums,
    );

    final ranked = _rankingEngine.rank(
      events: _eventsFor(plays: plays, chartType: ChartType.albums),
      historyByEntityId: historyByEntityId,
      includeObservedPeakInResult: false,
    );

    final entries = ranked
        .map(
          (result) => _entryFromRanked(
            result: result,
            entity: identityByAlbumId[result.entityId]!,
            isFinalized: true,
          ),
        )
        .toList(growable: false);

    return _snapshot(
      chartType: ChartType.albums,
      weekStartUtc: weekStartUtc,
      weekEndUtc: weekEndUtc,
      finalizedAt: finalizedAt,
      revision: revision,
      entries: entries,
    );
  }

  static List<ChartRankingEvent> _eventsFor({
    required List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
    required ChartType chartType,
  }) {
    return plays
        .map(
          (play) => ChartRankingEvent(
            entityId: switch (chartType) {
              ChartType.songs => play.songId,
              ChartType.albums => play.albumId,
            },
            qualifiedAt: play.qualifiedAt,
            sourceEventId: play.id,
          ),
        )
        .toList(growable: false);
  }

  static Map<String, ChartEntitySnapshot> _historicalIdentityByEntity({
    required List<QualifiedPlay> plays,
    required Map<String, PlaybackSession> sessionsById,
    required ChartType chartType,
  }) {
    final ordered = [...plays]
      ..sort((a, b) {
        final timeCompare = a.qualifiedAt.toUtc().compareTo(
          b.qualifiedAt.toUtc(),
        );

        if (timeCompare != 0) {
          return timeCompare;
        }

        return a.id.compareTo(b.id);
      });

    final identities = <String, ChartEntitySnapshot>{};

    for (final play in ordered) {
      final session = sessionsById[play.playbackSessionId]!;

      switch (chartType) {
        case ChartType.songs:
          identities[play.songId] = ChartEntitySnapshot(
            entityId: play.songId,
            title: session.songTitle,
            artistId: session.artistId,
            artistName: session.artistName,
            albumTitle: session.albumTitle,
          );

        case ChartType.albums:
          identities[play.albumId] = ChartEntitySnapshot(
            entityId: play.albumId,
            title: session.albumTitle,
            artistId: session.artistId,
            artistName: session.artistName,
          );
      }
    }

    return identities;
  }

  static ChartEntry _entryFromRanked({
    required ChartRankedEntity result,
    required ChartEntitySnapshot entity,
    required bool isFinalized,
  }) {
    return ChartEntry(
      entity: entity,
      rank: result.rank,
      qualifiedStreamCount: result.qualifiedStreamCount,
      lastQualifiedAt: result.lastQualifiedAt,
      previousFinalRank: result.previousFinalRank,
      peakRank: result.peakRank,
      peakAchievedAt: result.peakAchievedAt,
      weeksOnChart: _resolvedWeeksOnChart(
        existingWeeks: result.weeksOnChart,
        rank: result.rank,
        isFinalized: isFinalized,
      ),
      movement: result.movement,
    );
  }

  static ChartSnapshot _snapshot({
    required ChartType chartType,
    required DateTime weekStartUtc,
    required DateTime weekEndUtc,
    required DateTime? finalizedAt,
    required int revision,
    required List<ChartEntry> entries,
  }) {
    return ChartSnapshot(
      id: _snapshotId(chartType: chartType, weekStartUtc: weekStartUtc),
      chartType: chartType,
      weekStartUtc: weekStartUtc,
      weekEndUtc: weekEndUtc,
      finalizedAt: finalizedAt?.toUtc(),
      revision: revision,
      entries: List<ChartEntry>.unmodifiable(entries),
    );
  }

  static Map<String, ChartRankingHistory> _buildHistoryContext({
    required List<ChartSnapshot> history,
    required DateTime currentWeekStartUtc,
  }) {
    final mutable = <String, _MutableHistory>{};

    for (final snapshot in history) {
      final snapshotStartUtc = snapshot.weekStartUtc.toUtc();

      if (!snapshotStartUtc.isBefore(currentWeekStartUtc.toUtc())) {
        continue;
      }

      final isPreviousWeek =
          snapshot.weekEndUtc.toUtc() == currentWeekStartUtc.toUtc();

      for (final entry in snapshot.entries) {
        final entityId = entry.entity.entityId;

        final state = mutable.putIfAbsent(
          entityId,
          () => _MutableHistory(entityId),
        );

        // Peak history spans the full persisted Top 200. This lets Moments
        // distinguish a genuine first Top-200 appearance from a return to
        // territory previously reached outside the official Top 100.
        if (state.peakRank == null || entry.peakRank < state.peakRank!) {
          state.peakRank = entry.peakRank;
          state.peakAchievedAt = entry.peakAchievedAt;
        } else if (entry.peakRank == state.peakRank &&
            state.peakAchievedAt != null &&
            entry.peakAchievedAt.isBefore(state.peakAchievedAt!)) {
          state.peakAchievedAt = entry.peakAchievedAt;
        }
        if (isPreviousWeek) {
          state.previousExtendedRank = entry.rank;
        }

        // Official chart career accounting remains Top 100 only.
        if (!entry.isOfficiallyCharting) {
          continue;
        }

        state.hasEverCharted = true;
        state.finalizedWeeksOnChart++;

        if (isPreviousWeek) {
          state.previousFinalRank = entry.rank;
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      for (final state in mutable.values)
        state.entityId: ChartRankingHistory(
          entityId: state.entityId,
          finalizedWeeksOnChart: state.finalizedWeeksOnChart,
          hasEverCharted: state.hasEverCharted,
          previousFinalRank: state.previousFinalRank,
          previousExtendedRank: state.previousExtendedRank,
          peakRank: state.peakRank,
          peakAchievedAt: state.peakAchievedAt,
        ),
    };
  }

  static int _resolvedWeeksOnChart({
    required int existingWeeks,
    required int rank,
    required bool isFinalized,
  }) {
    if (!isFinalized || rank > ChartRankingEngine.officialChartLimit) {
      return existingWeeks;
    }

    return existingWeeks + 1;
  }

  static String _snapshotId({
    required ChartType chartType,
    required DateTime weekStartUtc,
  }) {
    return 'chart_${chartType.name}_${weekStartUtc.toUtc().millisecondsSinceEpoch}';
  }
}

class _MutableHistory {
  _MutableHistory(this.entityId);

  final String entityId;

  int finalizedWeeksOnChart = 0;
  bool hasEverCharted = false;

  int? previousFinalRank;

  int? previousExtendedRank;

  int? peakRank;
  DateTime? peakAchievedAt;
}

===== lib\features\charts\application\services\live_chart_moment_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../../library/application/repositories/library_query_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_entity_snapshot.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_type.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_event.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_live_detector.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_rank_observation.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/chart_moment_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/services/chart_week_resolver.dart';
import '../models/live_chart_moment_result.dart';
import 'chart_snapshot_builder_service.dart';

class LiveChartMomentService {
  const LiveChartMomentService({
    required ChartSnapshotBuilderService builder,
    required LibraryQueryRepository library,
    required ChartMomentRepository moments,
    ChartWeekResolver weekResolver = const ChartWeekResolver(),
    ChartMomentLiveDetector detector = const ChartMomentLiveDetector(),
  }) : _builder = builder,
       _library = library,
       _moments = moments,
       _weekResolver = weekResolver,
       _detector = detector;

  final ChartSnapshotBuilderService _builder;
  final LibraryQueryRepository _library;
  final ChartMomentRepository _moments;
  final ChartWeekResolver _weekResolver;
  final ChartMomentLiveDetector _detector;

  Future<LiveChartMomentResult> load({
    required ChartType chartType,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
    String? userId,
  }) async {
    final week = _weekResolver.resolve(referenceLocal);
    final observations = <ChartMomentRankObservation>[];

    final snapshot = await _builder.build(
      chartType: chartType,
      weekStartUtc: week.startUtc,
      weekEndUtc: week.endUtc,
      onRankObservation: observations.add,
    );

    final entities = await _entitySnapshots(chartType);
    final createdAt = DateTime.now().toUtc();

    final moments = <ChartMomentEvent>[];

    for (final observation in observations) {
      final entity = entities[observation.entityId];

      if (entity == null) {
        continue;
      }
      final detected = _detector.detectRankTransition(
        chartType: chartType,
        entity: entity,
        weekStartUtc: week.startUtc,
        weekEndUtc: week.endUtc,
        observation: observation,
        createdAt: createdAt,
        userId: userId,
      );

      moments.addAll(detected);
    }

    await _moments.saveAll(moments);

    return LiveChartMomentResult(
      snapshot: snapshot,
      moments: List.unmodifiable(moments),
    );
  }

  Future<Map<String, ChartEntitySnapshot>> _entitySnapshots(
    ChartType chartType,
  ) async {
    switch (chartType) {
      case ChartType.songs:
        final tracks = await _library.getTracks();

        return {
          for (final track in tracks)
            if (!track.isBlocked)
              track.songId: ChartEntitySnapshot(
                entityId: track.songId,
                title: track.title,
                artistId: track.artistId,
                artistName: track.artistName,
                albumTitle: track.albumTitle,
              ),
        };

      case ChartType.albums:
        final albums = await _library.getAlbums();

        return {
          for (final album in albums)
            album.albumId: ChartEntitySnapshot(
              entityId: album.albumId,
              title: album.title,
              artistId: album.artistId,
              artistName: album.artistName,
            ),
        };
    }
  }
}

===== lib\features\charts\application\services\live_chart_service.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import '../../domain/entities/chart_snapshot.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_type.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_view_size.dart';
import '../../domain/services/chart_week_resolver.dart';
import 'chart_snapshot_builder_service.dart';

class LiveChartService {
  const LiveChartService({
    required ChartSnapshotBuilderService builder,
    ChartWeekResolver weekResolver = const ChartWeekResolver(),
  }) : _builder = builder,
       _weekResolver = weekResolver;

  final ChartSnapshotBuilderService _builder;
  final ChartWeekResolver _weekResolver;

  Future<ChartSnapshot> load({
    required ChartType chartType,
    required DateTime referenceLocal,
    ChartViewSize viewSize = ChartViewSize.top100,
  }) async {
    final week = _weekResolver.resolve(referenceLocal);

    final snapshot = await _builder.build(
      chartType: chartType,
      weekStartUtc: week.startUtc,
      weekEndUtc: week.endUtc,
    );

    if (snapshot.entries.length <= viewSize.limit) {
      return snapshot;
    }

    return ChartSnapshot(
      id: snapshot.id,
      chartType: snapshot.chartType,
      weekStartUtc: snapshot.weekStartUtc,
      weekEndUtc: snapshot.weekEndUtc,
      finalizedAt: snapshot.finalizedAt,
      revision: snapshot.revision,
      entries: List.unmodifiable(snapshot.entries.take(viewSize.limit)),
    );
  }
}

===== lib\features\charts\data\repositories\sqlite_chart_moment_repository.dart =====
import '../../../../core/database/app_database.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_type.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_event.dart';
import '../../domain/moments/chart_moment_scope.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/chart_moment_repository.dart';
import '../daos/chart_moment_event_dao.dart';
import '../mappers/chart_moment_event_row_mapper.dart';

class SqliteChartMomentRepository implements ChartMomentRepository {
  const SqliteChartMomentRepository(this.database);

  final AppDatabase database;

  @override
  Future<void> saveAll(Iterable<ChartMomentEvent> events) {
    return database.transaction((transaction) async {
      final dao = ChartMomentEventDao(transaction);

      for (final event in events) {
        await dao.insertIgnoreDuplicate(ChartMomentEventRowMapper.toRow(event));
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<void> replaceFinalizedForWeek({
    required ChartType chartType,
    required DateTime weekStartUtc,
    required Iterable<ChartMomentEvent> events,
  }) async {
    final normalizedWeekStartUtc = weekStartUtc.toUtc();
    final replacement = List<ChartMomentEvent>.unmodifiable(events);

    for (final event in replacement) {
      if (event.scope != ChartMomentScope.finalized) {
        throw ArgumentError.value(
          event.scope,
          'events',
          'FINAL Moment replacement cannot contain live events.',
        );
      }

      if (event.chartType != chartType) {
        throw ArgumentError.value(
          event.chartType,
          'events',
          'FINAL Moment replacement cannot contain another chart type.',
        );
      }

      if (event.weekStartUtc.toUtc() != normalizedWeekStartUtc) {
        throw ArgumentError.value(
          event.weekStartUtc,
          'events',
          'FINAL Moment replacement cannot contain another chart week.',
        );
      }
    }

    await database.transaction((transaction) async {
      final dao = ChartMomentEventDao(transaction);

      await dao.deleteFinalizedForWeek(
        chartType: chartType.name,
        weekStartUtc: normalizedWeekStartUtc,
      );

      for (final event in replacement) {
        await dao.insertIgnoreDuplicate(ChartMomentEventRowMapper.toRow(event));
      }
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<ChartMomentEvent?> getById(String id) async {
    final db = await database.database;
    final row = await ChartMomentEventDao(db).findById(id);

    if (row == null) {
      return null;
    }

    return ChartMomentEventRowMapper.fromRow(row);
  }

  @override
  Future<List<ChartMomentEvent>> getRecent({int? limit}) async {
    final db = await database.database;

    final rows = await ChartMomentEventDao(db).findRecent(limit: limit);

    return List.unmodifiable(rows.map(ChartMomentEventRowMapper.fromRow));
  }
}

===== lib\features\charts\data\repositories\sqlite_chart_repository.dart =====
import '../../../../core/database/app_database.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/chart_snapshot.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_type.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/chart_repository.dart';
import '../daos/chart_entry_dao.dart';
import '../daos/chart_snapshot_dao.dart';
import '../mappers/chart_entry_row_mapper.dart';
import '../mappers/chart_snapshot_row_mapper.dart';

class SqliteChartRepository implements ChartRepository {
  const SqliteChartRepository(this.database);

  final AppDatabase database;

  @override
  Future<void> replaceSnapshot(ChartSnapshot snapshot) {
    return database.transaction((transaction) async {
      final snapshotDao = ChartSnapshotDao(transaction);
      final entryDao = ChartEntryDao(transaction);

      final existing = await snapshotDao.findForWeek(
        chartType: snapshot.chartType.name,
        weekStartUtc: snapshot.weekStartUtc,
      );

      if (existing != null) {
        final existingRevision = existing['revision']! as int;

        if (snapshot.revision < existingRevision) {
          throw StateError(
            'Cannot replace chart snapshot revision '
            '$existingRevision with older revision ${snapshot.revision}.',
          );
        }
      }

      final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();

      if (existing == null) {
        await snapshotDao.insert(
          ChartSnapshotRowMapper.toRow(
            snapshot: snapshot,
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
          ),
        );
      } else {
        final existingId = existing['id']! as String;

        if (existingId != snapshot.id) {
          await snapshotDao.deleteById(existingId);

          await snapshotDao.insert(
            ChartSnapshotRowMapper.toRow(
              snapshot: snapshot,
              createdAt: DateTime.parse(existing['created_at']! as String),
              updatedAt: now,
            ),
          );
        } else {
          await snapshotDao.update(
            ChartSnapshotRowMapper.toRow(
              snapshot: snapshot,
              createdAt: DateTime.parse(existing['created_at']! as String),
              updatedAt: now,
            ),
          );

          await entryDao.deleteForSnapshot(snapshot.id);
        }
      }

      await entryDao.insertAll(
        snapshot.entries.map(
          (entry) => ChartEntryRowMapper.toRow(
            snapshotId: snapshot.id,
            entry: entry,
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
          ),
        ),
      );
    });
  }

  @override
  Future<ChartSnapshot?> getById(String id) async {
    final db = await database.database;

    final snapshotDao = ChartSnapshotDao(db);
    final entryDao = ChartEntryDao(db);

    final snapshotRow = await snapshotDao.findById(id);

    if (snapshotRow == null) {
      return null;
    }

    return _hydrate(snapshotRow: snapshotRow, entryDao: entryDao);
  }

  @override
  Future<ChartSnapshot?> getForWeek({
    required ChartType chartType,
    required DateTime weekStartUtc,
  }) async {
    final db = await database.database;

    final snapshotDao = ChartSnapshotDao(db);
    final entryDao = ChartEntryDao(db);

    final snapshotRow = await snapshotDao.findForWeek(
      chartType: chartType.name,
      weekStartUtc: weekStartUtc,
    );

    if (snapshotRow == null) {
      return null;
    }

    return _hydrate(snapshotRow: snapshotRow, entryDao: entryDao);
  }

  @override
  Future<List<ChartSnapshot>> getHistory(
    ChartType chartType, {
    int? limit,
  }) async {
    final db = await database.database;

    final snapshotDao = ChartSnapshotDao(db);
    final entryDao = ChartEntryDao(db);

    final snapshotRows = await snapshotDao.findHistory(
      chartType.name,
      limit: limit,
    );

    final snapshots = <ChartSnapshot>[];

    for (final snapshotRow in snapshotRows) {
      snapshots.add(
        await _hydrate(snapshotRow: snapshotRow, entryDao: entryDao),
      );
    }

    return List<ChartSnapshot>.unmodifiable(snapshots);
  }

  static Future<ChartSnapshot> _hydrate({
    required Map<String, Object?> snapshotRow,
    required ChartEntryDao entryDao,
  }) async {
    final snapshotId = snapshotRow['id']! as String;

    final entryRows = await entryDao.findForSnapshot(snapshotId);

    final entries = entryRows
        .map(ChartEntryRowMapper.fromRow)
        .toList(growable: false);

    return ChartSnapshotRowMapper.fromRow(
      snapshotRow: snapshotRow,
      entries: entries,
    );
  }
}

===== lib\features\charts\domain\moments\chart_moment_lifetime_stream_service.dart =====
import '../../../analytics/domain/entities/qualified_play.dart';
import '../../../analytics/domain/repositories/qualified_play_repository.dart';
import '../entities/chart_snapshot.dart';
import '../models/chart_type.dart';

class ChartMomentLifetimeStreamService {
  const ChartMomentLifetimeStreamService({required this._qualifiedPlays});

  final QualifiedPlayRepository _qualifiedPlays;

  Future<Map<String, int>> load({required ChartSnapshot snapshot}) async {
    if (!snapshot.isFinalized) {
      throw ArgumentError.value(
        snapshot.finalizedAt,
        'snapshot',
        'Lifetime stream state requires a finalized chart snapshot.',
      );
    }

    return await loadBefore(
      chartType: snapshot.chartType,
      qualifiedBeforeUtc: snapshot.weekEndUtc,
    );
  }

  Future<Map<String, int>> loadBefore({
    required ChartType chartType,
    required DateTime qualifiedBeforeUtc,
  }) async {
    final plays = await _qualifiedPlays.getAll(
      qualifiedBefore: qualifiedBeforeUtc.toUtc(),
    );

    final totals = <String, int>{};

    for (final play in plays) {
      final entityId = _entityIdFor(play: play, chartType: chartType);

      totals.update(entityId, (current) => current + 1, ifAbsent: () => 1);
    }

    return Map<String, int>.unmodifiable(totals);
  }

  static String _entityIdFor({
    required QualifiedPlay play,
    required ChartType chartType,
  }) {
    switch (chartType) {
      case ChartType.songs:
        return play.songId;

      case ChartType.albums:
        return play.albumId;
    }
  }
}

===== lib\features\charts\presentation\controllers\charts_controller.dart =====
// ignore_for_file: prefer_initializing_formals

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../../library/application/models/library_album.dart';
import '../../../library/application/models/library_track.dart';
import '../../../library/application/repositories/library_query_repository.dart';
import '../../application/models/chart_moment_feed_item.dart';
import '../../application/services/chart_finalization_service.dart';
import '../../application/services/chart_moment_feed_aggregator.dart';
import '../../application/services/live_chart_service.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/chart_entry.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/chart_snapshot.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_type.dart';
import '../../domain/models/chart_view_size.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/chart_moment_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/chart_repository.dart';
import '../models/charts_presentation_mode.dart';

typedef ChartsControllerNow = DateTime Function();

class ChartsController extends ChangeNotifier {
  ChartsController({
    required LiveChartService liveCharts,
    required ChartFinalizationService finalization,
    required ChartRepository charts,
    required LibraryQueryRepository library,
    ChartMomentRepository? moments,
    ChartMomentFeedAggregator momentFeedAggregator =
        const ChartMomentFeedAggregator(),
    ChartsControllerNow? now,
  }) : _liveCharts = liveCharts,
       _finalization = finalization,
       _charts = charts,
       _library = library,
       _moments = moments,
       _momentFeedAggregator = momentFeedAggregator,
       _now = now ?? DateTime.now;

  final LiveChartService _liveCharts;
  final ChartFinalizationService _finalization;
  final ChartRepository _charts;
  final LibraryQueryRepository _library;
  final ChartMomentRepository? _moments;
  final ChartMomentFeedAggregator _momentFeedAggregator;
  final ChartsControllerNow _now;

  ChartType _chartType = ChartType.songs;
  ChartViewSize _viewSize = ChartViewSize.top100;

  ChartsPresentationMode _presentationMode = ChartsPresentationMode.charts;

  ChartSnapshot? _liveSnapshot;
  List<ChartSnapshot> _history = const [];

  List<ChartMomentFeedItem> _momentFeedItems = const [];
  Object? _momentFeedError;

  Map<String, LibraryTrack> _tracksById = const {};
  Map<String, LibraryAlbum> _albumsById = const {};

  int? _historyIndex;

  bool _isLoading = false;
  Object? _error;

  int _requestGeneration = 0;

  ChartType get chartType => _chartType;

  ChartViewSize get viewSize => _viewSize;

  ChartsPresentationMode get presentationMode => _presentationMode;

  bool get isShowingCharts =>
      _presentationMode == ChartsPresentationMode.charts;

  bool get isShowingMoments =>
      _presentationMode == ChartsPresentationMode.moments;

  bool get isLoading => _isLoading;

  Object? get error => _error;

  bool get hasError => _error != null;

  bool get isViewingHistory => _historyIndex != null;

  bool get isViewingLive => _historyIndex == null;

  List<ChartSnapshot> get history => _history;

  List<ChartMomentFeedItem> get momentFeedItems => _momentFeedItems;

  Object? get momentFeedError => _momentFeedError;

  bool get hasMomentFeedError => _momentFeedError != null;

  bool get hasMomentFeedItems => _momentFeedItems.isNotEmpty;

  ChartSnapshot? get liveSnapshot => _liveSnapshot;

  ChartSnapshot? get selectedHistoricalSnapshot {
    final index = _historyIndex;

    if (index == null || index < 0 || index >= _history.length) {
      return null;
    }

    return _history[index];
  }

  ChartSnapshot? get snapshot {
    final selected = selectedHistoricalSnapshot;

    if (selected != null) {
      return _limitSnapshot(selected);
    }

    return _liveSnapshot;
  }

  bool get hasSnapshot => snapshot != null;

  bool get isEmpty =>
      !_isLoading &&
      _error == null &&
      (snapshot == null || snapshot!.entries.isEmpty);

  bool get canGoToOlderWeek {
    if (_history.isEmpty) {
      return false;
    }

    final index = _historyIndex;

    if (index == null) {
      return true;
    }

    return index + 1 < _history.length;
  }

  bool get canGoToNewerWeek {
    final index = _historyIndex;

    if (index == null) {
      return false;
    }

    return index >= 0;
  }

  LibraryTrack? trackForEntry(ChartEntry entry) {
    if (_chartType != ChartType.songs) {
      return null;
    }

    return _tracksById[entry.entity.entityId];
  }

  LibraryAlbum? albumForEntry(ChartEntry entry) {
    if (_chartType != ChartType.albums) {
      return null;
    }

    return _albumsById[entry.entity.entityId];
  }

  bool isTrackInteractive(ChartEntry entry) {
    if (!isViewingLive) {
      return false;
    }

    final track = trackForEntry(entry);

    return track != null &&
        !track.isBlocked &&
        track.isAvailable &&
        track.filePath != null &&
        track.filePath!.trim().isNotEmpty;
  }

  bool isAlbumInteractive(ChartEntry entry) {
    return isViewingLive && albumForEntry(entry) != null;
  }

  void selectPresentationMode(ChartsPresentationMode value) {
    if (_presentationMode == value) {
      return;
    }

    _presentationMode = value;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  String? artworkPathForMoment(ChartMomentFeedItem item) {
    return switch (item.chartType) {
      ChartType.songs => _tracksById[item.entity.entityId]?.artworkPath,
      ChartType.albums => _albumsById[item.entity.entityId]?.artworkPath,
    };
  }

  String? relatedArtworkPathForMoment(ChartMomentFeedItem item) {
    final related = item.relatedEntity;

    if (related == null) {
      return null;
    }

    return switch (item.chartType) {
      ChartType.songs => _tracksById[related.entityId]?.artworkPath,
      ChartType.albums => _albumsById[related.entityId]?.artworkPath,
    };
  }

  Future<void> load() {
    if (_isLoading) {
      return Future<void>.value();
    }

    return _load(finalizeCompletedWeeks: true);
  }

  Future<void> refresh() {
    return _load(finalizeCompletedWeeks: true);
  }

  Future<void> selectChartType(ChartType value) async {
    if (_chartType == value) {
      return;
    }

    _chartType = value;
    _historyIndex = null;

    await _load(finalizeCompletedWeeks: false);
  }

  Future<void> selectViewSize(ChartViewSize value) async {
    if (_viewSize == value) {
      return;
    }

    _viewSize = value;

    if (isViewingHistory) {
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }

    await _loadLiveOnly();
  }

  void showLive() {
    if (_historyIndex == null) {
      return;
    }

    _historyIndex = null;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void showOlderWeek() {
    if (_history.isEmpty) {
      return;
    }

    final index = _historyIndex;

    if (index == null) {
      _historyIndex = 0;
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }

    if (index + 1 >= _history.length) {
      return;
    }

    _historyIndex = index + 1;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void showNewerWeek() {
    final index = _historyIndex;

    if (index == null) {
      return;
    }

    if (index == 0) {
      _historyIndex = null;
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }

    _historyIndex = index - 1;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  bool selectHistoricalWeek(DateTime weekStartUtc) {
    final target = weekStartUtc.toUtc();

    final index = _history.indexWhere(
      (snapshot) => snapshot.weekStartUtc.toUtc() == target,
    );

    if (index < 0) {
      return false;
    }

    _historyIndex = index;
    notifyListeners();

    return true;
  }

  Future<void> _load({required bool finalizeCompletedWeeks}) async {
    final generation = ++_requestGeneration;

    _isLoading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final referenceLocal = _now();

      if (finalizeCompletedWeeks) {
        await _finalization.finalizeCompletedWeeks(
          referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
        );
      }

      final live = await _liveCharts.load(
        chartType: _chartType,
        referenceLocal: referenceLocal,
        viewSize: _viewSize,
      );

      final history = await _charts.getHistory(_chartType);

      final tracks = await _library.getTracks();
      final albums = await _library.getAlbums();

      var momentFeedItems = _momentFeedItems;
      Object? momentFeedError;

      final moments = _moments;

      if (moments != null) {
        try {
          final rawMomentEvents = await moments.getRecent();

          momentFeedItems = _momentFeedAggregator.aggregate(rawMomentEvents);
        } catch (error) {
          momentFeedError = error;
        }
      }

      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _liveSnapshot = live;
      _history = List<ChartSnapshot>.unmodifiable(history);

      _tracksById = {for (final track in tracks) track.songId: track};

      _albumsById = {for (final album in albums) album.albumId: album};

      _momentFeedItems = List<ChartMomentFeedItem>.unmodifiable(
        momentFeedItems,
      );
      _momentFeedError = momentFeedError;

      final historyIndex = _historyIndex;

      if (historyIndex != null && historyIndex >= _history.length) {
        _historyIndex = _history.isEmpty ? null : _history.length - 1;
      }

      _error = null;
    } catch (error) {
      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _error = error;
    } finally {
      if (generation == _requestGeneration) {
        _isLoading = false;
        notifyListeners();
      }
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadLiveOnly() async {
    final generation = ++_requestGeneration;

    _isLoading = true;
    _error = null;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      final live = await _liveCharts.load(
        chartType: _chartType,
        referenceLocal: _now(),
        viewSize: _viewSize,
      );

      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _liveSnapshot = live;
      _error = null;
    } catch (error) {
      if (generation != _requestGeneration) {
        return;
      }

      _error = error;
    } finally {
      if (generation == _requestGeneration) {
        _isLoading = false;
        notifyListeners();
      }
    }
  }

  ChartSnapshot _limitSnapshot(ChartSnapshot source) {
    if (source.entries.length <= _viewSize.limit) {
      return source;
    }

    return ChartSnapshot(
      id: source.id,
      chartType: source.chartType,
      weekStartUtc: source.weekStartUtc,
      weekEndUtc: source.weekEndUtc,
      finalizedAt: source.finalizedAt,
      revision: source.revision,
      entries: List.unmodifiable(source.entries.take(_viewSize.limit)),
    );
  }
}

class SonusApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const SonusApp({

  });

  final PlaybackEngineFactory? playbackEngineFactory;
  final AppDatabase? libraryDatabase;

                ),

            ),
          ),
        ),

}

===== .\lib\features\shell\presentation\pages\app_shell_page.dart =====
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

import '../../../charts/presentation/pages/charts_page.dart';
import '../../../home/presentation/pages/home_page.dart';
import '../../../import/presentation/pages/imports_page.dart';
import '../../../library/presentation/pages/library_page.dart';
import '../../../library/presentation/pages/tracks_page.dart';
import '../../../playlists/presentation/pages/playlists_page.dart';
import '../../../search/presentation/pages/search_page.dart';
import '../../../settings/presentation/pages/settings_page.dart';
import '../../application/controllers/app_shell_controller.dart';
import '../../domain/models/app_destination.dart';
import '../widgets/shell_playback_slot.dart';

class AppShellPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const AppShellPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<AppShellPage> createState() => _AppShellPageState();
}

class _AppShellPageState extends State<AppShellPage>
    with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  late final AppShellController _shellController;
  late final PageController _pageController;
  late final TabController _tabController;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();

    _shellController = AppShellController();

    _pageController = PageController(
      initialPage: _shellController.currentIndex,
    );

    _tabController = TabController(
      length: AppDestination.values.length,
      vsync: this,
      initialIndex: _shellController.currentIndex,
    );
  }

  void _selectDestination(AppDestination destination) {
    _shellController.selectDestination(destination);

    if (_tabController.index != destination.index) {
      _tabController.animateTo(destination.index);
    }

    if (!_pageController.hasClients) {
      return;
    }

    _pageController.animateToPage(
      destination.index,
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
      curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
    );
  }

  void _handlePageChanged(int index) {
    _shellController.selectIndex(index);

    if (_tabController.index != index) {
      _tabController.animateTo(index);
    }
  }

  void _openSearch() {
    Navigator.of(context).push(
      MaterialPageRoute<void>(
        builder: (_) => const Scaffold(
          body: SearchPage(),
          bottomNavigationBar: ShellPlaybackSlot(),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _openImports() {
    Navigator.of(
      context,
    ).push(MaterialPageRoute<void>(builder: (_) => const ImportsPage()));
  }

  void _openSettings() {
    _selectDestination(AppDestination.settings);
  }

  Widget _buildDestinationPage(AppDestination destination) {
    final Widget page = switch (destination) {
      AppDestination.tracks => const TracksPage(),
      AppDestination.library => const LibraryPage(),
      AppDestination.home => const HomePage(),
      AppDestination.playlists => const PlaylistsPage(),
      AppDestination.settings => const SettingsPage(),
      AppDestination.hot100 => const ChartsPage(),
    };

    return KeyedSubtree(
      key: PageStorageKey<String>('app-page-${destination.name}'),
      child: page,
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return ChangeNotifierProvider<AppShellController>.value(
      value: _shellController,
      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(
          titleSpacing: 16,
          title: const _SonusAppTitle(),
          actions: [
            IconButton(
              key: const ValueKey<String>('shell-search-button'),
              tooltip: 'Search',
              onPressed: _openSearch,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.search_rounded),
            ),
            IconButton(
              key: const ValueKey<String>('shell-import-button'),
              tooltip: 'Imports',
              onPressed: _openImports,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.add_rounded),
            ),
            IconButton(
              key: const ValueKey<String>('shell-settings-button'),
              tooltip: 'Settings',
              onPressed: _openSettings,
              icon: const Icon(Icons.settings_rounded),
            ),
          ],
          bottom: TabBar(
            controller: _tabController,
            isScrollable: true,
            tabs: [
              for (final destination in AppDestination.values)
                Tab(
                  key: ValueKey<String>('app-tab-${destination.name}'),
                  text: destination.label,
                ),
            ],
            onTap: (index) {
              _selectDestination(AppDestination.values[index]);
            },
          ),
        ),
        body: PageView(
          controller: _pageController,
          onPageChanged: _handlePageChanged,
          children: [
            for (final destination in AppDestination.values)
              _buildDestinationPage(destination),
          ],
        ),
        bottomNavigationBar: const ShellPlaybackSlot(),
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _tabController.dispose();
    _pageController.dispose();
    _shellController.dispose();

    super.dispose();
  }
}

class _SonusAppTitle extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SonusAppTitle();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return const Row(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        Icon(Icons.graphic_eq_rounded, key: ValueKey<String>('sonus-app-mark')),
        SizedBox(width: 8),
        Text('Sonus', key: ValueKey<String>('sonus-app-title')),
      ],
    );
  }
}
